    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto world = worldMatrix(id);
    if (!world) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: !world", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    glm::mat4 child(1);
    if (face.independence)
        child = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), face.transform.localBasis)
              * detail::transformMatrix(face.transform);
    const auto result = *world * child * face.localAnimationMatrix;
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Scene::faceWorldMatrix","Face world transform overflow.","Inspect local face animation and entity transforms.",id);return {};}
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::lineWorldMatrix(LineId id) const {
    if (id >= lines_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::lineWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    if (lines_[id].owner) return worldMatrix(*lines_[id].owner);
    const auto global = motions_.layerMatrices(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    return global ? std::optional<glm::mat4>(global->parent * global->local) : std::nullopt;
}
inline bool Scene::bindEntity(EntityId id, LayerId layer) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::bindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_[id] = layer;
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::unbindEntity(EntityId id) {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::unbindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_.erase(id);
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> Scene::getEntityLayer(EntityId id) const {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return {};
    const auto found = entityLayers_.find(id);
    if (found == entityLayers_.end() || !motions_.getLayer(found->second)) return {};
    return found->second;
}

inline bool Scene::setTransform(EntityId id, const Transform3D& transform) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].transform = transform;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceTransform(
    EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
    const Transform3D& transform, bool independence
) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    face.transform = transform;
    face.independence = independence;
    return true;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addLine(
    const glm::vec3& start, const glm::vec3& end,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end)
        || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addLine", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end) || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    const LineId id = lines_.size();
    lines_.push_back({ start, end, color, owner });
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return id;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addVector(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    return addLine(origin, origin + vector, color, owner);
}

inline std::optional<std::array<LineId, 3>> Scene::addBasis(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::mat3& basisVectors,
    const glm::vec3& axisLengths, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(origin)
        || !positiveLengths(axisLengths)
        || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(origin) || !positiveLengths(axisLengths) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        if (!finiteVector(basisVectors[i])
            || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f
            || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])) {
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(basisVectors[i]) || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
        }
    }
    // 사전 검증 후 세 선을 함께 등록한다. 실패 입력은 장면을 변경하지 않는다.
    const std::array<GlColor, 3> colors{
        GlColor{ 1.0f, 0.2f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 1.0f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 0.4f, 1.0f }
    };
    std::array<LineId, 3> ids{};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        ids[i] = lines_.size();
        lines_.push_back({
            origin, origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i], colors[i], owner });
    }
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return ids;
}

inline bool Scene::setVector(LineId id, const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector) {
    if (id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin)
        || !finiteVector(origin + vector)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVector", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin) || !finiteVector(origin + vector)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    lines_[id].start = origin;
    lines_[id].end = origin + vector;
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return true;
}

inline void Scene::clear() {
    motions_.clear();
    entityLayers_.clear();
    if (components_.empty() && lines_.empty()) {
        return;
    }
    polyId_.clear();
    components_.clear();
    cubeFaceId_.clear();
    tetraFaceId_.clear();
    pyramidFaceId_.clear();
    lines_.clear();
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
}

inline bool DiagramSystem::validTransform(const Transform3D& transform) {
    return finiteVector(transform.basis) && finiteVector(transform.localBasis)
        && finiteVector(transform.scale)
        && finiteVector(transform.rotation[0]) && finiteVector(transform.rotation[1])
        && finiteVector(transform.rotation[2])
        && std::abs(glm::determinant(transform.rotation) - 1.0f) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[0] - glm::vec3(1,0,0)) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[1] - glm::vec3(0,1,0)) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[2] - glm::vec3(0,0,1)) < 0.01f
        && finiteMatrix(detail::transformMatrix(transform));
}

inline bool DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(const Polyhedron& polyhedron) {
    if (polyhedron.faces.empty()) return false;
    for (const auto& face : polyhedron.faces)
        if (!detail::validMesh(face) || !validTransform(face.transform)) return false;
    return true;
}

inline Polyhedron DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape shape, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) {
    Polyhedron polyhedron;
    if (!positiveLengths(sideLengths)) {
        return polyhedron;
    }
    polyhedron.shape = shape;
    if (shape == GLShape::Cube) {
        const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f;
        const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f;
        const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f;
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(6);
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, z }, { x, -y, z }, { x, y, z }, { -x, y, z } },
            { .9f, .3f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, y, -z }, { x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, z }, { -x, y, z }, { -x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { x, -y, z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, y, -z }, { x, y, z } },
            { .9f, .8f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, y, z }, { x, y, z }, { x, y, -z }, { -x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .8f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, z }, { -x, -y, z } },
            { .8f, .3f, .9f });
    } else if (shape == GLShape::Pyramid) {
        const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f;
        const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f;
        const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f;
        const glm::vec3 frontLeft{ -x, -y, z };
        const glm::vec3 frontRight{ x, -y, z };
        const glm::vec3 backRight{ x, -y, -z };
        const glm::vec3 backLeft{ -x, -y, -z };
        const glm::vec3 apex{ 0.0f, y, 0.0f };
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(5);
        // 앞, 오른쪽, 뒤, 왼쪽, 바닥. 삼각형과 사각형은 바깥쪽 winding이다.
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { frontLeft, frontRight, apex }, { .9f, .3f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { frontRight, backRight, apex }, { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { backRight, backLeft, apex }, { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { backLeft, frontLeft, apex }, { .9f, .8f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { backLeft, backRight, frontRight, frontLeft }, { .8f, .3f, .9f });
    } else if (shape == GLShape::Tetra) {
        // 원점에서 직교하는 세 모서리를 만들고, 무게중심을 로컬 원점으로 옮긴다.
        const glm::vec3 center = sideLengths * 0.25f;
        const glm::vec3 origin = -center;
        const glm::vec3 x = glm::vec3(sideLengths.x, 0.0f, 0.0f) - center;
        const glm::vec3 y = glm::vec3(0.0f, sideLengths.y, 0.0f) - center;
        const glm::vec3 z = glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, sideLengths.z) - center;
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(4);
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, y, x }, { .9f, .4f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, x, z }, { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, z, y }, { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { x, y, z }, { .9f, .8f, .3f });
    }
    return polyhedron;
}

inline Model DiagramSystem::makeSphere(float radius,unsigned slices,unsigned stacks) {
    Model model;
    if(!std::isfinite(radius)||radius<=0||slices<3||slices>512||stacks<2||stacks>256)return model;
    Mesh mesh;mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,radius,0});
    constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846;
    for(unsigned row=1;row<stacks;++row) {
        const double latitude=pi*row/stacks;
        for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) {
            const double longitude=2*pi*col/slices;
            mesh.localVertices.push_back(radius*glm::vec3(std::sin(latitude)*std::cos(longitude),
                std::cos(latitude),std::sin(latitude)*std::sin(longitude)));
        }
    }
    const auto bottom=std::uint32_t(mesh.localVertices.size());
    mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,-radius,0});
    for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) {
        const auto next=(col+1)%slices;
        mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{0,1+next,1+col});
        for(unsigned row=0;row+2<stacks;++row) {
            const auto a=1+row*slices+col,b=1+row*slices+next,c=a+slices,d=b+slices;
            mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,c,b,d,c});
        }
        const auto a=1+(stacks-2)*slices+col,b=1+(stacks-2)*slices+next;
        mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,bottom});
    }
    for(const auto& p:mesh.localVertices)mesh.vertexNormals.push_back(glm::normalize(p));
    model.faces.push_back(std::move(mesh));
    return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{};
}
inline Model DiagramSystem::makeCone(float radius,float height,unsigned slices) {
    Model model;
    if(!std::isfinite(radius)||!std::isfinite(height)||radius<=0||height<=0||slices<3||slices>512)return model;
    constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846;
    // Separate sides/base keep the bottom rim sharp for normals and outlines.
    Mesh sides,base;sides.localVertices.push_back({0,height*.5f,0});
    base.localVertices.push_back({0,-height*.5f,0});
    for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) {
        const double a=2*pi*i/slices;
        const glm::vec3 p{radius*float(std::cos(a)),-height*.5f,radius*float(std::sin(a))};
        sides.localVertices.push_back(p);base.localVertices.push_back(p);
    }
    for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) {
        const auto a=1+i,b=1+(i+1)%slices;
        sides.triangleIndices.insert(sides.triangleIndices.end(),{0,b,a});
        base.triangleIndices.insert(base.triangleIndices.end(),{0,a,b});
    }
    recalculateNormals(sides);recalculateNormals(base);
    model.faces={std::move(sides),std::move(base)};
    return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{};
}

inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(const Transform3D& transform) {
    return detail::transformMatrix(transform);
}

inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::faceModelMatrix(const Component_Poly& component,
                                         const PlaneFigure& face) {
    return detail::faceMatrix(component, face);
}

inline bool CameraSystem::validCamera(const Camera& camera) {
    const glm::vec3 direction = camera.target - camera.position;
    const glm::vec3 right = glm::cross(direction, camera.up);
    return finiteVector(camera.position) && finiteVector(camera.target)
        && finiteVector(camera.up) && finiteVector(direction) && finiteVector(right)
        && std::isfinite(glm::dot(direction, direction))
        && std::isfinite(glm::dot(right, right))
        && glm::dot(direction, direction) > 0.000001f
        && glm::dot(right, right) > 0.000001f
        && std::isfinite(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees)
        && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees > 0.0f && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees < 180.0f
        && std::isfinite(camera.nearPlane) && std::isfinite(camera.farPlane)
        && camera.nearPlane > 0.0f && camera.farPlane > camera.nearPlane
        && std::isfinite(camera.orthographicHalfExtent)
        && camera.orthographicHalfExtent > 0.0f;
}

inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::viewMatrix(const Camera& camera) {
    return glm::lookAt(camera.position, camera.target, camera.up);
}

inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(const Camera& camera, float aspect) {
    if (camera.orthographic) {
        const float halfWidth = camera.orthographicHalfExtent * (aspect >= 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f);
        const float halfHeight = camera.orthographicHalfExtent / (aspect < 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f);
        return glm::ortho(-halfWidth, halfWidth, -halfHeight, halfHeight,
                          camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane);
    }
    return glm::perspective(glm::radians(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees), aspect,
                            camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane);
}


inline void Scene::rebuildFaceIds() {
    cubeFaceId_.clear(); tetraFaceId_.clear(); pyramidFaceId_.clear();
    for (EntityId id : polyId_) {
        const auto& model = components_[id].polyhedron;
        auto* ids = model.shape == GLShape::Cube ? &cubeFaceId_
            : model.shape == GLShape::Tetra ? &tetraFaceId_
            : model.shape == GLShape::Pyramid ? &pyramidFaceId_ : nullptr;
        if(ids) for(std::size_t f=0; f<model.faces.size(); ++f) ids->push_back({id,f});
    }
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addModel(const Model& model, const Transform3D& transform,
                                             PivotMode pivot, const glm::vec3& customPivot) {
    if(!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addModel", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    auto initial=transform;
    if(pivot==PivotMode::BottomCenter) initial.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model);
    else if(pivot==PivotMode::Custom) initial.localBasis=customPivot;
    auto id=push_entity(model,initial);
    if(id) components_[*id].pivotMode=pivot;
    return id;
}
inline bool Scene::setModel(EntityId id, const Model& model) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto box=detail::bounds(model);
    if(!detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=components_[id];
    replacement.polyhedron=model;replacement.sideLengths=box.second-box.first;replacement.transformOnly=false;
    if(replacement.pivotMode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)
        replacement.transform.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model);
    components_[id]=std::move(replacement);
    rebuildFaceIds();geometryRevision_=nextGeometryRevision();return true;
}
inline const Mesh* Scene::getMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex) const {
    const auto* c=getComponent(id);
    return c && meshIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size()?&c->polyhedron.faces[meshIndex]:nullptr;
}
inline bool Scene::setMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const Mesh& mesh) {
    if(!getMesh(id,meshIndex)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMesh", "Rejected condition: !getMesh(id,meshIndex)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    Model replacement=components_[id].polyhedron;
    replacement.faces[meshIndex]=mesh;
    return setModel(id,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setMeshVertices(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,
                                   const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
                                   const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& triangles) {
    const auto* original=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!original)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMeshVertices", "Rejected condition: !original", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*original;
    const auto geometry=Mesh::fromVertices(vertices,triangles);
    replacement.localVertices=geometry.localVertices;replacement.vertexNormals=geometry.vertexNormals;
    replacement.texcoords=geometry.texcoords;replacement.vertexColors=geometry.vertexColors;
    replacement.triangleIndices=triangles;replacement.shape=GLFace::Mesh;
    replacement.outlineIndices.clear();
    if(replacement.outline==OutlineMode::Explicit) replacement.outline=OutlineMode::None;
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setVertexPosition(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,std::size_t vertexIndex,
                                     const glm::vec3& position) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);
    if(!mesh||vertexIndex>=mesh->localVertices.size()||!detail::finiteVector(position))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVertexPosition", "Rejected condition: !mesh||vertexIndex>=mesh->localVertices.size()||!detail::finiteVector(position)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.localVertices[vertexIndex]=position;
    recalculateNormals(replacement);
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setTriangleIndices(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,
                                      const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& indices) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTriangleIndices", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.shape=GLFace::Mesh;replacement.triangleIndices=indices;
    if(!detail::validMesh(replacement))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTriangleIndices", "Rejected condition: !detail::validMesh(replacement)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    recalculateNormals(replacement);return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::generateTexcoords(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const UVOptions& options) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex); if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::generateTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;
    return graphics::generateTexcoords(replacement,options) && setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::transformTexcoords(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const glm::vec2& scale,
                                     const glm::vec2& offset, float rotationDegrees) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex); if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::transformTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;
    return graphics::transformTexcoords(replacement,scale,offset,rotationDegrees) && setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setTexcoords(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const std::vector<glm::vec2>& uvs) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.texcoords=uvs;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setVertexNormals(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const std::vector<glm::vec3>& normals) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVertexNormals", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.vertexNormals=normals;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setMaterial(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const Material& material) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMaterial", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.material=material;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setOutline(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,OutlineMode mode,
                              const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& explicitEdges) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setOutline", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.outline=mode;replacement.outlineIndices=explicitEdges;
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setLocalBasis(EntityId id,PivotMode mode,const glm::vec3& customPivot) {
    if(id>=components_.size()||!detail::finiteVector(customPivot))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setLocalBasis", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()||!detail::finiteVector(customPivot)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto& c=components_[id];
    if(c.transformOnly && mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setLocalBasis", "Rejected condition: c.transformOnly && mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    if(mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)c.transform.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(c.polyhedron);
    else if(mode==PivotMode::Custom)c.transform.localBasis=customPivot;
    c.pivotMode=mode;return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setVisible(EntityId id,bool visible) {
    if(id>=components_.size())return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVisible", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    components_[id].visible=visible;return true;
}
inline bool Scene::splitFace(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex) {
    if(!getMesh(id,faceIndex))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::splitFace", "Rejected condition: !getMesh(id,faceIndex)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    // The same face remains under its parent; vertices and indices are already face-local storage.
    auto& face=components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    if(!face.independence) {face.transform=Transform3D{};face.independence=true;}
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setFaceSRT(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex,const Transform3D& transform) {
    return setFaceTransform(id,faceIndex,transform,true);
}
} // namespace graphics

GPU 업로드: transform 변경과 정점 변경의 비용 차이

상자가 이동할 때 setTransform은 CPU 컴포넌트의 변환을 바꾼다. 같은 상자의 정점 배열을 매 프레임 다시 만들 필요는 없다. 반면 setMeshVertices나 setModel로 형상을 바꾸면 geometry revision이 변하고 Renderer가 다음 렌더링에서 업로드를 다시 준비한다. 서로 다른 Scene을 같은 revision 숫자만으로 혼동하지 않도록 Scene 포인터도 비교한다.

upload는 CPU Mesh를 GPU 정점 배열과 인덱스 배열로 펼친다. VAO는 attribute 형식과 버퍼 연결 상태를 보관하고, VBO는 정점 바이트를, EBO는 삼각형·외곽선 인덱스를 보관한다. DrawRange는 한 엔티티의 한 면이 어느 인덱스 구간을 그릴지 기록한다. 프로그램을 만들었다는 사실과 어떤 VAO/EBO가 현재 바인딩되어 있는지는 별개의 상태다.

실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Renderer.inl
#pragma once
#include "Shaders.h"
#include "../dependencies/glm/glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>

namespace graphics {
inline std::string readTextFile(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    std::ifstream input(path,std::ios::binary);
    if(!input) return {};
    std::ostringstream buffer;buffer<<input.rdbuf();auto text=buffer.str();
    if(text.compare(0,3,"\xEF\xBB\xBF")==0) text.erase(0,3);
    return text;
}
namespace detail {
inline GLuint compileShader(GLenum kind,const std::string& sourceText,const std::filesystem::path& sourcePath) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Shader, "compileShader");
    auto& api=gl();const auto shader=api.CreateShader(kind);
    if(!shader) { Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glCreateShader", "Shader allocation failed."); return 0; }
    const char* text=sourceText.c_str();api.ShaderSource(shader,1,&text,nullptr);api.CompileShader(shader);
    GLint success=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_COMPILE_STATUS,&success);
    if(!success) {
        GLint length=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&length);
        std::string log(std::max(length,1), '\0');api.GetShaderInfoLog(shader,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, kind==GL_VERTEX_SHADER?"vertex shader":"fragment shader", "GLSL file="+sourcePath.u8string()+"\n"+log,
            "Read the compiler line number; check GLSL version, declarations and shader inputs/outputs.");
        api.DeleteShader(shader);return 0;
    }
    return shader;
}
struct GpuVertex { float position[3], color[3], uv[2], normal[3]; };
inline GLuint uploadTexture(const TextureImage& image,bool repeat) {
    auto& api=gl();GLuint id=0;api.GenTextures(1,&id);
    if(!id){Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset,"glGenTextures","Texture allocation failed.");return 0;}
    api.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);api.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,id);
    GLint previousAlignment=4;api.GetIntegerv(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,&previousAlignment);
    api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,1);
    api.TexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0,GL_RGBA8,GLsizei(image.width),GLsizei(image.height),0,GL_RGBA,GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE,image.rgba.data());
    api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,previousAlignment);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    if(!checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "uploadTexture")) {api.DeleteTextures(1,&id);return 0;}
    return id;
}
} // namespace detail

inline bool Renderer::initShader(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    const auto vertexText=vertexPath.empty()?std::string(detail::vertexShader):readTextFile(vertexPath);
    const auto fragmentText=fragmentPath.empty()?std::string(detail::fragmentShader):readTextFile(fragmentPath);
    if(vertexText.empty()||fragmentText.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "Renderer::initShader",
        "Shader source is empty or unreadable: vertex=" + vertexPath.u8string() + "; fragment=" + fragmentPath.u8string(),
        "Use embedded shaders (empty shaderDirectory), or copy both GLSL files to the configured project-relative directory.");
    // /utf-8 /FC encodes __FILE__ as UTF-8, not the Windows ANSI code page.
    const auto embeddedSource=std::filesystem::u8path(__FILE__).parent_path()/"Shaders.h";
    const auto vertex=detail::compileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER,vertexText,vertexPath.empty()?embeddedSource:vertexPath);if(!vertex)return false;
    const auto fragment=detail::compileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER,fragmentText,fragmentPath.empty()?embeddedSource:fragmentPath);
    if(!fragment){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);return false;}
    shaderProgramID_=gl.CreateProgram();
    if(!shaderProgramID_){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader,"glCreateProgram","Program allocation failed.");}
    gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,vertex);gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,fragment);gl.LinkProgram(shaderProgramID_);
    gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);
    GLint success=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_LINK_STATUS,&success);
    if(!success) {
        GLint size=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&size);
        std::string log(std::max(size,1),'\0');gl.GetProgramInfoLog(shaderProgramID_,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glLinkProgram", log, "Match vertex outputs and fragment inputs; inspect the linker log.");
    }
    mvpLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uMVP");
    modelLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uModel");
    normalLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uNormalMatrix");
    textureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTexture");
    hasTextureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uHasTexture");
    lightingLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLighting");
    lightDirectionLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightDirection");
    lightColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightColor");
    overrideLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOverrideColor");
    outlineColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOutlineColor");
    tintLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTint");
    return mvpLocation_>=0 || Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glGetUniformLocation", "Required uMVP uniform is missing or optimized away.");
}
inline bool Renderer::initialize(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {
    detail::gl().load();destroy();
    if(!initShader(vertexPath,fragmentPath)){destroy();return false;}
    auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.GenVertexArrays(1,&vao_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&vbo_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&ebo_);
    if(!vao_||!vbo_||!ebo_){destroy();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize", "VAO/VBO/EBO allocation failed.");}
    gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_);
    using V=detail::GpuVertex;
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(0,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,position)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(1,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,color)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(2,2,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,uv)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(3,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,normal)));
    for(GLuint i=0;i<4;++i)gl.EnableVertexAttribArray(i);
    gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize vertex layout");
}
inline bool Renderer::upload(const Scene& scene,bool textureMode) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload");
    std::vector<detail::GpuVertex> vertices;
    std::vector<GLuint> indices;std::vector<DrawRange> ranges;
    TextureMap stagedTextures;auto& gl=detail::gl();
    auto cleanup=[&](){for(const auto& item:stagedTextures)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);};
    try {
        for(auto id:scene.getIds()) {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(id);if(c&&c->transformOnly)continue;
            if(!c||!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(c->polyhedron)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload", "Missing component or invalid mesh, indices, colors, UVs or normals.", "Inspect the entity's mesh data before GPU upload.", id);}
            for(std::size_t faceIndex=0;faceIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size();++faceIndex) {
                const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
                if(vertices.size()+face.localVertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()) {cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");}
                const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size());
                Mesh normals=face;if(normals.vertexNormals.empty())recalculateNormals(normals);
                for(std::size_t i=0;i<face.localVertices.size();++i) {
                    const auto p=face.localVertices[i];const auto n=normals.vertexNormals[i];
                    auto color=face.vertexColors.empty()?face.color:face.vertexColors[i];
                    color.red*=face.material.color.red;color.green*=face.material.color.green;color.blue*=face.material.color.blue;
                    const auto uv=face.texcoords.empty()?glm::vec2(0):face.texcoords[i];
                    vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{color.red,color.green,color.blue},{uv.x,uv.y},{n.x,n.y,n.z}});
                }
                const auto triangles=detail::triangles(face);
                const auto outline=meshOutline(face,face.outline==OutlineMode::None?OutlineMode::FeatureEdges:face.outline);
                if(triangles.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()||outline.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Draw count exceeds GLsizei range.");}
                DrawRange range;range.entityId=id;range.faceIndex=faceIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=GLsizei(triangles.size());
                for(auto index:triangles)indices.push_back(first+index);
                range.outlineFirstIndex=indices.size();range.outlineCount=GLsizei(outline.size());
                for(auto index:outline)indices.push_back(first+index);
                ranges.push_back(range);
                if(textureMode&&face.material.useTexture&&stagedTextures.find(face.material.texture)==stagedTextures.end()) {
                    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "before uploadTexture")){cleanup();return false;}
                    const auto texture=detail::uploadTexture(*face.material.texture,face.material.repeatTexture);
                    if(!texture){cleanup();return false;}
                    try{stagedTextures.emplace(face.material.texture,texture);}catch(...){gl.DeleteTextures(1,&texture);throw;}
                }
            }
        }
        for(std::size_t lineIndex=0;lineIndex<scene.getLines().size();++lineIndex) {
            const auto& line=scene.getLines()[lineIndex];
            if(vertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()-2){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload lines","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");}
            const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size());
            for(auto p:{line.start,line.end})vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{line.color.red,line.color.green,line.color.blue},{0,0},{0,0,0}});
            DrawRange range;range.lineId=lineIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=2;
            ranges.push_back(range);indices.insert(indices.end(),{first,first+1});
        }
        if(vertices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(detail::GpuVertex)
            ||indices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(GLuint)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Buffer byte size exceeds GLsizeiptr range.");}
        if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "before buffer upload")){cleanup();return false;}
        gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_);
        gl.BufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(vertices.size()*sizeof(detail::GpuVertex)),vertices.empty()?nullptr:vertices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW);
        gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_);
        gl.BufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(indices.size()*sizeof(GLuint)),indices.empty()?nullptr:indices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW);
        gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0);
        if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "glBufferData")){cleanup();return false;}
        for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);
        textures_=std::move(stagedTextures);drawRanges_=std::move(ranges);return true;
    } catch(...) {cleanup();throw;}
}
inline bool Renderer::draw(const Scene& scene,const glm::mat4& view,const glm::mat4& projection,
                           const RenderOptions& options) const {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw");
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(view)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "View or projection matrix contains NaN/Inf.");
    for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
        glm::mat4 matrix(1);
        if(range.lineId) {
            const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world;
        } else {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);
            const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world;
        }
        if(!detail::finiteMatrix(matrix)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection*view*matrix))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "World or MVP matrix contains NaN/Inf.", "Check parent transforms, scales and motion samples.", range.entityId);
    }
    auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.UseProgram(shaderProgramID_);gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);
    auto cull=[&](const Mesh* face,const glm::mat4& model) {
        const auto mode=face && face->cull!=CullMode::Inherit?face->cull:options.cull;
        gl.FrontFace(glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model))<0?GL_CW:GL_CCW);
        if(!face || mode==CullMode::None || mode==CullMode::Inherit) gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);
        else {gl.Enable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.CullFace(mode==CullMode::Front?GL_FRONT:GL_BACK);}
    };
    gl.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);gl.Uniform1i(textureLocation_,0);
    gl.Uniform3f(lightDirectionLocation_,options.lightDirection.x,options.lightDirection.y,options.lightDirection.z);
    gl.Uniform3f(lightColorLocation_,options.lightColor.red,options.lightColor.green,options.lightColor.blue);
    gl.Uniform3f(outlineColorLocation_,options.outlineColor.red,options.outlineColor.green,options.outlineColor.blue);
    if(options.wireframe && options.depthTest) {
        // All surfaces write depth first, so a later object's front face hides earlier back edges.
        gl.ColorMask(GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE);
        for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
            if(range.lineId)continue;
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);
            const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex];
            if(!c->visible||!face.visible)continue;
            const auto model=*scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);
            cull(&face,model);
            const auto matrix=projection*view*model;
            gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(matrix));
            gl.DrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
        }
        gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    }
    for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
        glm::mat4 model(1);const Mesh* face=nullptr;GlColor tint;
        if(range.lineId) {
            const auto& line=scene.getLines()[*range.lineId];
            if(line.owner){const auto* owner=scene.getComponent(*line.owner);if(!owner||!owner->visible)continue;}
            const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;model=*world;
        } else {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);if(!c||!c->visible)continue;
            face=&c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex];if(!face->visible)continue;tint=c->tint;
            const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;model=*world;
        }
        const auto mvp=projection*view*model;
        cull(face,model);
        const float determinant=glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model));
        const auto normalMatrix=std::isfinite(determinant)&&std::abs(determinant)>1e-12f
            ?glm::transpose(glm::inverse(glm::mat3(model))):glm::mat3(1);
        gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(mvp));
        gl.UniformMatrix4fv(modelLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(model));
        gl.UniformMatrix3fv(normalLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(normalMatrix));
        gl.Uniform3f(tintLocation_,tint.red,tint.green,tint.blue);
        gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,face&&options.lighting);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0);
        const bool textured=face&&options.textures&&face->material.useTexture;
        gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,textured);
        if(textured){gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,textures_.at(face->material.texture));
            gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);}
        else gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0);
        if(!face||!options.wireframe)
        {
            gl.DrawElements(face?GL_TRIANGLES:GL_LINES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
            if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "glDrawElements")) {
                gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return false;
            }
        }
        if(face&&(options.wireframe||face->outline!=OutlineMode::None)) {
            gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,options.wireframe?0:1);
            gl.DepthFunc(GL_LEQUAL);gl.DepthMask(GL_FALSE);
            gl.DrawElements(GL_LINES,range.outlineCount,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.outlineFirstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
            gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);
        }
    }
    gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0);gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0);gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "Renderer::draw outlines and cleanup");
}
inline bool Renderer::renderPass(const Scene& scene,const RenderPass& pass,int width,int height) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass");
    const auto& camera=pass.camera;const auto& options=pass.options;const auto& viewport=pass.viewport;
    if(!shaderProgramID_||!vao_)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderPass","Renderer is not initialized.");
    if(width<=0||height<=0)return true;
    if(viewport.x<0 || viewport.y<0 || viewport.x>width || viewport.y>height || viewport.width<0 || viewport.height<0
        || viewport.width>width-viewport.x || viewport.height>height-viewport.y)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass","Viewport lies outside the framebuffer.");
    if(viewport.width==0 || viewport.height==0) return true;
    if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera)||!detail::finiteVector(options.lightDirection)
        ||glm::length(options.lightDirection)<1e-12f||!detail::validColor(options.lightColor)
        ||!detail::validColor(options.outlineColor)
        ||!std::isfinite(options.background.r)||!std::isfinite(options.background.g)
        ||!std::isfinite(options.background.b)||!std::isfinite(options.background.a)
        ||(options.cull!=CullMode::Inherit && options.cull!=CullMode::None && options.cull!=CullMode::Back && options.cull!=CullMode::Front))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Renderer::renderPass","Invalid camera, light, color or culling mode.","Check near/far planes, FOV, target/up directions, positive orthographic extent and finite render options.");
    if(uploadedScene_!=&scene||uploadedRevision_!=scene.getGeometryRevision()||uploadedTextures_!=options.textures) {
        if(!upload(scene,options.textures))return false;
        uploadedScene_=&scene;uploadedRevision_=scene.getGeometryRevision();uploadedTextures_=options.textures;
    }
    auto& gl=detail::gl();if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "before Renderer::render"))return false;
    gl.Viewport(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height);gl.LineWidth(1);
    if(options.depthTest)gl.Enable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);else gl.Disable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);
    gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.Disable(GL_BLEND);gl.Enable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
    gl.Scissor(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height);
    gl.PolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK,GL_FILL);gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    gl.ClearColor(options.background.r,options.background.g,options.background.b,options.background.a);
    const GLbitfield mask=(pass.clearColor?GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT:0)|(pass.clearDepth?GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT:0);
    if(mask) gl.Clear(mask);
    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"viewport/scissor/clear")) {gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);return false;}
    const bool success=draw(scene,CameraSystem::viewMatrix(camera),CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(camera,float(viewport.width)/viewport.height),options);
    gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.FrontFace(GL_CCW);
    return success;
}
inline bool Renderer::render(const Scene& scene,const Camera& camera,int width,int height,const RenderOptions& options) {
    RenderPass pass;pass.camera=camera;pass.options=options;pass.viewport={0,0,width,height};
    return renderPass(scene,pass,width,height);
}
inline bool Renderer::renderViews(const Scene& scene,const std::vector<RenderPass>& passes,int width,int height) {
    if(width<=0 || height<=0) return true;
    if(passes.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews","Need at least one render pass.");
    if(!shaderProgramID_ || !vao_) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderViews","Renderer is not initialized.");
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Viewport(0,0,width,height);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    gl.ClearColor(.08f,.08f,.1f,1);gl.Clear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT|GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews full-frame clear")) return false;
    for(const auto& pass:passes) if(!renderPass(scene,pass,width,height)) return false;
    return true;
}
inline void Renderer::destroy() {
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    if(ebo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&ebo_);if(vbo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&vbo_);if(vao_)gl.DeleteVertexArrays(1,&vao_);
    if(shaderProgramID_)gl.DeleteProgram(shaderProgramID_);
    for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);
    textures_.clear();drawRanges_.clear();ebo_=vbo_=vao_=shaderProgramID_=0;
    uploadedScene_=nullptr;uploadedRevision_=std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max();uploadedTextures_=false;
}
} // namespace graphics

드로우·셰이더: 행렬과 법선은 어디서 소비되는가?

Renderer::draw는 객체별 M과 카메라 V/P로 uMVP를 계산한다. 정점 셰이더는 로컬 정점에 uMVP를 곱해 클립 좌표를 출력한다. 정점의 위치가 바뀌었다는 것과 픽셀이 화면에 보인다는 것은 다르다. 이후 조립·클리핑·원근 나눗셈·뷰포트·래스터화를 통과해야 프래그먼트 셰이더가 실행될 수 있다.

비균일 배율이 있는 모델의 법선은 위치와 같은 4×4 곱셈만으로 변환하면 면에 수직인 성질이 깨질 수 있다. draw에서 준비한 normal matrix를 정점 셰이더가 적용한다. 프래그먼트 셰이더는 색·선택적 텍스처·방향광 N·L 명암을 계산한다. 깊이 검사는 카메라 기준 가림을 해결한다. 이 기본 셰이더는 다른 물체가 바닥에 드리우는 shadow map을 계산하지 않는다.

실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Shaders.h
#pragma once
namespace graphics::detail {
inline constexpr char vertexShader[] = R"GLSL(#version 330 core
layout(location=0) in vec3 vPos;
layout(location=1) in vec3 vColor;
layout(location=2) in vec2 vTexCoord;
layout(location=3) in vec3 vNormal;
uniform mat4 uMVP;
uniform mat4 uModel;
uniform mat3 uNormalMatrix;
out vec3 outColor;
out vec2 TexCoord;
out vec3 Normal;
void main() {
    gl_Position = uMVP * vec4(vPos, 1.0);
    outColor = vColor;
    TexCoord = vTexCoord;
    Normal = uNormalMatrix * vNormal;
}
)GLSL";
inline constexpr char fragmentShader[] = R"GLSL(#version 330 core
in vec3 outColor;
in vec2 TexCoord;
in vec3 Normal;
uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform bool uHasTexture;
uniform bool uLighting;
uniform vec3 uLightDirection;
uniform vec3 uLightColor;
uniform bool uOverrideColor;
uniform vec3 uOutlineColor;
uniform vec3 uTint;
out vec4 FragColor;
void main() {
    if (uOverrideColor) { FragColor = vec4(uOutlineColor, 1.0); return; }
    vec4 color = vec4(outColor * uTint, 1.0);
    if (uHasTexture) color *= texture(uTexture, TexCoord);
    if (uLighting && length(Normal) > 0.0)
        color.rgb *= (0.25 + 0.75 * max(dot(normalize(Normal), normalize(uLightDirection)), 0.0)) * uLightColor;
    FragColor = color;
}
)GLSL";
}
실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Renderer.inl
#pragma once
#include "Shaders.h"
#include "../dependencies/glm/glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>

namespace graphics {
inline std::string readTextFile(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    std::ifstream input(path,std::ios::binary);
    if(!input) return {};
    std::ostringstream buffer;buffer<<input.rdbuf();auto text=buffer.str();
    if(text.compare(0,3,"\xEF\xBB\xBF")==0) text.erase(0,3);
    return text;
}
namespace detail {
inline GLuint compileShader(GLenum kind,const std::string& sourceText,const std::filesystem::path& sourcePath) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Shader, "compileShader");
    auto& api=gl();const auto shader=api.CreateShader(kind);
    if(!shader) { Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glCreateShader", "Shader allocation failed."); return 0; }
    const char* text=sourceText.c_str();api.ShaderSource(shader,1,&text,nullptr);api.CompileShader(shader);
    GLint success=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_COMPILE_STATUS,&success);
    if(!success) {
        GLint length=0;api.GetShaderiv(shader,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&length);
        std::string log(std::max(length,1), '\0');api.GetShaderInfoLog(shader,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());
        Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, kind==GL_VERTEX_SHADER?"vertex shader":"fragment shader", "GLSL file="+sourcePath.u8string()+"\n"+log,
            "Read the compiler line number; check GLSL version, declarations and shader inputs/outputs.");
        api.DeleteShader(shader);return 0;
    }
    return shader;
}
struct GpuVertex { float position[3], color[3], uv[2], normal[3]; };
inline GLuint uploadTexture(const TextureImage& image,bool repeat) {
    auto& api=gl();GLuint id=0;api.GenTextures(1,&id);
    if(!id){Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset,"glGenTextures","Texture allocation failed.");return 0;}
    api.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);api.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,id);
    GLint previousAlignment=4;api.GetIntegerv(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,&previousAlignment);
    api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,1);
    api.TexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0,GL_RGBA8,GLsizei(image.width),GLsizei(image.height),0,GL_RGBA,GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE,image.rgba.data());
    api.PixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT,previousAlignment);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,GL_LINEAR);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    api.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,repeat?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
    if(!checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "uploadTexture")) {api.DeleteTextures(1,&id);return 0;}
    return id;
}
} // namespace detail

inline bool Renderer::initShader(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    const auto vertexText=vertexPath.empty()?std::string(detail::vertexShader):readTextFile(vertexPath);
    const auto fragmentText=fragmentPath.empty()?std::string(detail::fragmentShader):readTextFile(fragmentPath);
    if(vertexText.empty()||fragmentText.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "Renderer::initShader",
        "Shader source is empty or unreadable: vertex=" + vertexPath.u8string() + "; fragment=" + fragmentPath.u8string(),
        "Use embedded shaders (empty shaderDirectory), or copy both GLSL files to the configured project-relative directory.");
    // /utf-8 /FC encodes __FILE__ as UTF-8, not the Windows ANSI code page.
    const auto embeddedSource=std::filesystem::u8path(__FILE__).parent_path()/"Shaders.h";
    const auto vertex=detail::compileShader(GL_VERTEX_SHADER,vertexText,vertexPath.empty()?embeddedSource:vertexPath);if(!vertex)return false;
    const auto fragment=detail::compileShader(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER,fragmentText,fragmentPath.empty()?embeddedSource:fragmentPath);
    if(!fragment){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);return false;}
    shaderProgramID_=gl.CreateProgram();
    if(!shaderProgramID_){gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader,"glCreateProgram","Program allocation failed.");}
    gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,vertex);gl.AttachShader(shaderProgramID_,fragment);gl.LinkProgram(shaderProgramID_);
    gl.DeleteShader(vertex);gl.DeleteShader(fragment);
    GLint success=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_LINK_STATUS,&success);
    if(!success) {
        GLint size=0;gl.GetProgramiv(shaderProgramID_,GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH,&size);
        std::string log(std::max(size,1),'\0');gl.GetProgramInfoLog(shaderProgramID_,GLsizei(log.size()),nullptr,log.data());
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glLinkProgram", log, "Match vertex outputs and fragment inputs; inspect the linker log.");
    }
    mvpLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uMVP");
    modelLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uModel");
    normalLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uNormalMatrix");
    textureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTexture");
    hasTextureLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uHasTexture");
    lightingLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLighting");
    lightDirectionLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightDirection");
    lightColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uLightColor");
    overrideLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOverrideColor");
    outlineColorLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uOutlineColor");
    tintLocation_=gl.GetUniformLocation(shaderProgramID_,"uTint");
    return mvpLocation_>=0 || Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Shader, "glGetUniformLocation", "Required uMVP uniform is missing or optimized away.");
}
inline bool Renderer::initialize(const std::filesystem::path& vertexPath,const std::filesystem::path& fragmentPath) {
    detail::gl().load();destroy();
    if(!initShader(vertexPath,fragmentPath)){destroy();return false;}
    auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.GenVertexArrays(1,&vao_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&vbo_);gl.GenBuffers(1,&ebo_);
    if(!vao_||!vbo_||!ebo_){destroy();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize", "VAO/VBO/EBO allocation failed.");}
    gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_);
    using V=detail::GpuVertex;
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(0,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,position)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(1,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,color)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(2,2,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,uv)));
    gl.VertexAttribPointer(3,3,GL_FLOAT,GL_FALSE,sizeof(V),reinterpret_cast<void*>(offsetof(V,normal)));
    for(GLuint i=0;i<4;++i)gl.EnableVertexAttribArray(i);
    gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::initialize vertex layout");
}
inline bool Renderer::upload(const Scene& scene,bool textureMode) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload");
    std::vector<detail::GpuVertex> vertices;
    std::vector<GLuint> indices;std::vector<DrawRange> ranges;
    TextureMap stagedTextures;auto& gl=detail::gl();
    auto cleanup=[&](){for(const auto& item:stagedTextures)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);};
    try {
        for(auto id:scene.getIds()) {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(id);if(c&&c->transformOnly)continue;
            if(!c||!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(c->polyhedron)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "Renderer::upload", "Missing component or invalid mesh, indices, colors, UVs or normals.", "Inspect the entity's mesh data before GPU upload.", id);}
            for(std::size_t faceIndex=0;faceIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size();++faceIndex) {
                const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
                if(vertices.size()+face.localVertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()) {cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");}
                const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size());
                Mesh normals=face;if(normals.vertexNormals.empty())recalculateNormals(normals);
                for(std::size_t i=0;i<face.localVertices.size();++i) {
                    const auto p=face.localVertices[i];const auto n=normals.vertexNormals[i];
                    auto color=face.vertexColors.empty()?face.color:face.vertexColors[i];
                    color.red*=face.material.color.red;color.green*=face.material.color.green;color.blue*=face.material.color.blue;
                    const auto uv=face.texcoords.empty()?glm::vec2(0):face.texcoords[i];
                    vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{color.red,color.green,color.blue},{uv.x,uv.y},{n.x,n.y,n.z}});
                }
                const auto triangles=detail::triangles(face);
                const auto outline=meshOutline(face,face.outline==OutlineMode::None?OutlineMode::FeatureEdges:face.outline);
                if(triangles.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()||outline.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLsizei>::max()){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Draw count exceeds GLsizei range.");}
                DrawRange range;range.entityId=id;range.faceIndex=faceIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=GLsizei(triangles.size());
                for(auto index:triangles)indices.push_back(first+index);
                range.outlineFirstIndex=indices.size();range.outlineCount=GLsizei(outline.size());
                for(auto index:outline)indices.push_back(first+index);
                ranges.push_back(range);
                if(textureMode&&face.material.useTexture&&stagedTextures.find(face.material.texture)==stagedTextures.end()) {
                    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Asset, "before uploadTexture")){cleanup();return false;}
                    const auto texture=detail::uploadTexture(*face.material.texture,face.material.repeatTexture);
                    if(!texture){cleanup();return false;}
                    try{stagedTextures.emplace(face.material.texture,texture);}catch(...){gl.DeleteTextures(1,&texture);throw;}
                }
            }
        }
        for(std::size_t lineIndex=0;lineIndex<scene.getLines().size();++lineIndex) {
            const auto& line=scene.getLines()[lineIndex];
            if(vertices.size()>std::numeric_limits<GLuint>::max()-2){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload lines","Vertex count exceeds the GPU index range.");}
            const auto first=GLuint(vertices.size());
            for(auto p:{line.start,line.end})vertices.push_back({{p.x,p.y,p.z},{line.color.red,line.color.green,line.color.blue},{0,0},{0,0,0}});
            DrawRange range;range.lineId=lineIndex;range.firstIndex=indices.size();range.count=2;
            ranges.push_back(range);indices.insert(indices.end(),{first,first+1});
        }
        if(vertices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(detail::GpuVertex)
            ||indices.size()>std::size_t(std::numeric_limits<GLsizeiptr>::max())/sizeof(GLuint)){cleanup();return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::upload","Buffer byte size exceeds GLsizeiptr range.");}
        if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "before buffer upload")){cleanup();return false;}
        gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,vbo_);
        gl.BufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(vertices.size()*sizeof(detail::GpuVertex)),vertices.empty()?nullptr:vertices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW);
        gl.BindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,ebo_);
        gl.BufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER,GLsizeiptr(indices.size()*sizeof(GLuint)),indices.empty()?nullptr:indices.data(),GL_STATIC_DRAW);
        gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.BindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER,0);
        if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput, "glBufferData")){cleanup();return false;}
        for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);
        textures_=std::move(stagedTextures);drawRanges_=std::move(ranges);return true;
    } catch(...) {cleanup();throw;}
}
inline bool Renderer::draw(const Scene& scene,const glm::mat4& view,const glm::mat4& projection,
                           const RenderOptions& options) const {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw");
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(view)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "View or projection matrix contains NaN/Inf.");
    for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
        glm::mat4 matrix(1);
        if(range.lineId) {
            const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world;
        } else {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);
            const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;matrix=*world;
        }
        if(!detail::finiteMatrix(matrix)||!detail::finiteMatrix(projection*view*matrix))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "Renderer::draw", "World or MVP matrix contains NaN/Inf.", "Check parent transforms, scales and motion samples.", range.entityId);
    }
    auto& gl=detail::gl();gl.UseProgram(shaderProgramID_);gl.BindVertexArray(vao_);
    auto cull=[&](const Mesh* face,const glm::mat4& model) {
        const auto mode=face && face->cull!=CullMode::Inherit?face->cull:options.cull;
        gl.FrontFace(glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model))<0?GL_CW:GL_CCW);
        if(!face || mode==CullMode::None || mode==CullMode::Inherit) gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);
        else {gl.Enable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.CullFace(mode==CullMode::Front?GL_FRONT:GL_BACK);}
    };
    gl.ActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);gl.Uniform1i(textureLocation_,0);
    gl.Uniform3f(lightDirectionLocation_,options.lightDirection.x,options.lightDirection.y,options.lightDirection.z);
    gl.Uniform3f(lightColorLocation_,options.lightColor.red,options.lightColor.green,options.lightColor.blue);
    gl.Uniform3f(outlineColorLocation_,options.outlineColor.red,options.outlineColor.green,options.outlineColor.blue);
    if(options.wireframe && options.depthTest) {
        // All surfaces write depth first, so a later object's front face hides earlier back edges.
        gl.ColorMask(GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE,GL_FALSE);
        for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
            if(range.lineId)continue;
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);
            const auto& face=c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex];
            if(!c->visible||!face.visible)continue;
            const auto model=*scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);
            cull(&face,model);
            const auto matrix=projection*view*model;
            gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(matrix));
            gl.DrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
        }
        gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    }
    for(const auto& range:drawRanges_) {
        glm::mat4 model(1);const Mesh* face=nullptr;GlColor tint;
        if(range.lineId) {
            const auto& line=scene.getLines()[*range.lineId];
            if(line.owner){const auto* owner=scene.getComponent(*line.owner);if(!owner||!owner->visible)continue;}
            const auto world=scene.lineWorldMatrix(*range.lineId);if(!world)return false;model=*world;
        } else {
            const auto* c=scene.getComponent(range.entityId);if(!c||!c->visible)continue;
            face=&c->polyhedron.faces[range.faceIndex];if(!face->visible)continue;tint=c->tint;
            const auto world=scene.faceWorldMatrix(range.entityId,range.faceIndex);if(!world)return false;model=*world;
        }
        const auto mvp=projection*view*model;
        cull(face,model);
        const float determinant=glm::determinant(glm::mat3(model));
        const auto normalMatrix=std::isfinite(determinant)&&std::abs(determinant)>1e-12f
            ?glm::transpose(glm::inverse(glm::mat3(model))):glm::mat3(1);
        gl.UniformMatrix4fv(mvpLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(mvp));
        gl.UniformMatrix4fv(modelLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(model));
        gl.UniformMatrix3fv(normalLocation_,1,GL_FALSE,glm::value_ptr(normalMatrix));
        gl.Uniform3f(tintLocation_,tint.red,tint.green,tint.blue);
        gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,face&&options.lighting);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0);
        const bool textured=face&&options.textures&&face->material.useTexture;
        gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,textured);
        if(textured){gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,textures_.at(face->material.texture));
            gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            gl.TexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D,GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T,face->material.repeatTexture?GL_REPEAT:GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);}
        else gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0);
        if(!face||!options.wireframe)
        {
            gl.DrawElements(face?GL_TRIANGLES:GL_LINES,range.count,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.firstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
            if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "glDrawElements")) {
                gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return false;
            }
        }
        if(face&&(options.wireframe||face->outline!=OutlineMode::None)) {
            gl.Uniform1i(hasTextureLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(lightingLocation_,0);gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,options.wireframe?0:1);
            gl.DepthFunc(GL_LEQUAL);gl.DepthMask(GL_FALSE);
            gl.DrawElements(GL_LINES,range.outlineCount,GL_UNSIGNED_INT,
                reinterpret_cast<const void*>(range.outlineFirstIndex*sizeof(GLuint)));
            gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);
        }
    }
    gl.Uniform1i(overrideLocation_,0);gl.BindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D,0);gl.BindVertexArray(0);gl.UseProgram(0);return detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster, "Renderer::draw outlines and cleanup");
}
inline bool Renderer::renderPass(const Scene& scene,const RenderPass& pass,int width,int height) {
    Diagnostics::trace(PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass");
    const auto& camera=pass.camera;const auto& options=pass.options;const auto& viewport=pass.viewport;
    if(!shaderProgramID_||!vao_)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderPass","Renderer is not initialized.");
    if(width<=0||height<=0)return true;
    if(viewport.x<0 || viewport.y<0 || viewport.x>width || viewport.y>height || viewport.width<0 || viewport.height<0
        || viewport.width>width-viewport.x || viewport.height>height-viewport.y)
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderPass","Viewport lies outside the framebuffer.");
    if(viewport.width==0 || viewport.height==0) return true;
    if(!CameraSystem::validCamera(camera)||!detail::finiteVector(options.lightDirection)
        ||glm::length(options.lightDirection)<1e-12f||!detail::validColor(options.lightColor)
        ||!detail::validColor(options.outlineColor)
        ||!std::isfinite(options.background.r)||!std::isfinite(options.background.g)
        ||!std::isfinite(options.background.b)||!std::isfinite(options.background.a)
        ||(options.cull!=CullMode::Inherit && options.cull!=CullMode::None && options.cull!=CullMode::Back && options.cull!=CullMode::Front))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Renderer::renderPass","Invalid camera, light, color or culling mode.","Check near/far planes, FOV, target/up directions, positive orthographic extent and finite render options.");
    if(uploadedScene_!=&scene||uploadedRevision_!=scene.getGeometryRevision()||uploadedTextures_!=options.textures) {
        if(!upload(scene,options.textures))return false;
        uploadedScene_=&scene;uploadedRevision_=scene.getGeometryRevision();uploadedTextures_=options.textures;
    }
    auto& gl=detail::gl();if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer, "before Renderer::render"))return false;
    gl.Viewport(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height);gl.LineWidth(1);
    if(options.depthTest)gl.Enable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);else gl.Disable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);
    gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.Disable(GL_BLEND);gl.Enable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
    gl.Scissor(viewport.x,viewport.y,viewport.width,viewport.height);
    gl.PolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK,GL_FILL);gl.DepthFunc(GL_LESS);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    gl.ClearColor(options.background.r,options.background.g,options.background.b,options.background.a);
    const GLbitfield mask=(pass.clearColor?GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT:0)|(pass.clearDepth?GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT:0);
    if(mask) gl.Clear(mask);
    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"viewport/scissor/clear")) {gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);return false;}
    const bool success=draw(scene,CameraSystem::viewMatrix(camera),CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(camera,float(viewport.width)/viewport.height),options);
    gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Disable(GL_CULL_FACE);gl.FrontFace(GL_CCW);
    return success;
}
inline bool Renderer::render(const Scene& scene,const Camera& camera,int width,int height,const RenderOptions& options) {
    RenderPass pass;pass.camera=camera;pass.options=options;pass.viewport={0,0,width,height};
    return renderPass(scene,pass,width,height);
}
inline bool Renderer::renderViews(const Scene& scene,const std::vector<RenderPass>& passes,int width,int height) {
    if(width<=0 || height<=0) return true;
    if(passes.empty()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews","Need at least one render pass.");
    if(!shaderProgramID_ || !vao_) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::VertexInput,"Renderer::renderViews","Renderer is not initialized.");
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    gl.Disable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);gl.Viewport(0,0,width,height);gl.DepthMask(GL_TRUE);gl.ColorMask(GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE,GL_TRUE);
    gl.ClearColor(.08f,.08f,.1f,1);gl.Clear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT|GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
    if(!detail::checkGlAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Framebuffer,"Renderer::renderViews full-frame clear")) return false;
    for(const auto& pass:passes) if(!renderPass(scene,pass,width,height)) return false;
    return true;
}
inline void Renderer::destroy() {
    auto& gl=detail::gl();
    if(ebo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&ebo_);if(vbo_)gl.DeleteBuffers(1,&vbo_);if(vao_)gl.DeleteVertexArrays(1,&vao_);
    if(shaderProgramID_)gl.DeleteProgram(shaderProgramID_);
    for(const auto& item:textures_)gl.DeleteTextures(1,&item.second);
    textures_.clear();drawRanges_.clear();ebo_=vbo_=vao_=shaderProgramID_=0;
    uploadedScene_=nullptr;uploadedRevision_=std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::max();uploadedTextures_=false;
}
} // namespace graphics

다중 뷰: 왜 상태를 한 번만 갱신하는가?

실습 22와 standard의 세 뷰는 같은 Scene을 원근·평면·정면 카메라로 반복해 그린다. 한 프레임에서 update를 세 번 실행하면 뷰마다 다른 시간의 자세가 보이므로, 시간 진행은 run의 update 단계에서 한 번 수행하고 렌더 패스는 읽기만 한다.

RenderPass.viewport는 framebuffer의 왼쪽 아래 원점 픽셀이다. Picking의 PickViewport는 왼쪽 위 원점이므로 마우스 좌표를 대응할 때 Y 방향을 바꿔야 한다. 렌더 패스의 viewport/scissor와 clear 플래그는 어떤 픽셀을 그리거나 지울지 정한다. 같은 창의 한 컨텍스트에서 그리며, 뷰마다 독립 창이나 모션 시계를 만들지 않는다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;
    path.durationSeconds = 6;
    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon({
            {1, -.5f, 0},
            {2.4f, -.4f, 0},
            {2.1f, .6f, 0},
            {1.4f, .8f, 0},
            {.9f, .2f, 0}
        });
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;
            return center + glm::vec3(
                float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)),
                float(.6 * std::sin(angle)),
                float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({
            {1, -.5f, 0},
            {2.4f, -.5f, 0},
            {2, .6f, 0}
        });
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";
    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
