        if (!std::isfinite(length) || length <= 1e-12) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::polyline", "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(length) || length <= 1e-12", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), PathFunction{});
        distances.push_back(distances.back() + length);
    }
    return [points = std::move(points), distances = std::move(distances)](double progress) {
        const double distance = std::clamp(progress, 0.0, 1.0) * distances.back();
        auto end = std::upper_bound(distances.begin(), distances.end(), distance);
        const std::size_t i = end == distances.end() ? distances.size() - 1
                                                   : std::size_t(end - distances.begin());
        const double weight = (distance - distances[i - 1]) / (distances[i] - distances[i - 1]);
        return glm::vec3(glm::dvec3(points[i - 1]) * (1 - weight)
                       + glm::dvec3(points[i]) * weight);
    };
}
inline PathFunction PathSystem::polygon(std::vector<glm::vec3> points) {
    return polyline(std::move(points), true);
}
inline PathFunction PathSystem::rectangle(const glm::vec3& center, const glm::vec3& u,
                                         const glm::vec3& v) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::rectangle", "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(center) || !detail::motionPlane(u, v)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), PathFunction{});
    const auto a = detail::motionUnitVector(u), b = detail::motionUnitVector(v);
    if (std::abs(glm::dot(*a, *b)) > 1e-5f) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform, "PathSystem::rectangle", "Rejected condition: std::abs(glm::dot(*a, *b)) > 1e-5f", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), PathFunction{});
    return polygon({center - u - v, center + u - v, center + u + v, center - u + v});
}
inline double PathSystem::linear(double progress) { return progress; }
inline double PathSystem::smoothStep(double progress) {
    return progress * progress * (3 - 2 * progress);
}
} // namespace graphics

한 물체의 모션 구성·편집·샘플링

상자가 2초 이동한 뒤 1초 회전하는 모션을 편집한다. append는 단계를 배열에 더할 뿐 시작 시간을 뒤로 밀지 않는다. 순차 실행은 then을 사용한다.

변수·객체 역할. Motion은 단계들의 값 묶음이다. MotionStep의 콜백이 받는 초는 해당 단계가 시작된 이후의 시간이다. sample(2.5)는 dt=.5가 아니라 모션 시작 후 2.5초의 자세를 요구한다.

새로 작성한 사용 사례 · 단편별 전제는 본문 참조 · 빌드하지 않음

std::optional<graphics::Motion> makeMotion() {
    graphics::Motion motion;
    graphics::LinearMotion move; move.to={2,0,0}; move.durationSeconds=2;
    if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move))) return {};
    graphics::RotationMotion spin; spin.degreesPerSecond=90;
    auto rotate=graphics::Motion::rotation(spin); rotate.durationSeconds=1;
    if(!motion.then(rotate)) return {};
    auto fixed=graphics::Motion::fixed(glm::mat4(1));
    if(!motion.insert(0,fixed)||!motion.replace(0,fixed)||!motion.erase(0)) return {};
    const auto& steps=motion.getSteps(); (void)steps;
    auto pose=motion.sample(2.5); if(!pose) return {};
    graphics::PathMotion path; path.path=graphics::PathSystem::circle({0,0,0},1);
    graphics::Motion looping;
    if(!looping.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) return {};
    graphics::PendulumMotion sway; sway.periodSeconds=3;
    graphics::Motion pendulum;
    if(!pendulum.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway))) return {};
    return motion;
}

유한 종료 시간이 없는 루프 단계 뒤에는 then을 사용할 수 없다. Once 단계는 종료 자세를 유지한다. 진자는 중력 적분이 아니라 지정 주기의 sin 각도 함수다.

실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Motion.inl
#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace graphics {
inline bool Motion::validStep(const MotionStep& step) {
    return bool(step.transform) && std::isfinite(step.startSeconds) && step.startSeconds >= 0
        && (!step.durationSeconds || (std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds)
            && *step.durationSeconds >= 0
            && std::isfinite(step.startSeconds + *step.durationSeconds)));
}
inline bool Motion::append(const MotionStep& step) { return insert(steps_.size(), step); }
inline bool Motion::insert(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {
    if (index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::insert", "Rejected condition: index > steps_.size() || !validStep(step)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_.insert(steps_.begin() + index, step);
    return true;
}
inline bool Motion::replace(std::size_t index, const MotionStep& step) {
    if (index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::replace", "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size() || !validStep(step)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_[index] = step;
    return true;
}
inline bool Motion::erase(std::size_t index) {
    if (index >= steps_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::erase", "Rejected condition: index >= steps_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    steps_.erase(steps_.begin() + index);
    return true;
}
inline bool Motion::then(MotionStep step) {
    double end = 0;
    for (const auto& current : steps_) {
        if (!current.durationSeconds) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::then", "Rejected condition: !current.durationSeconds", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
        end = std::max(end, current.startSeconds + *current.durationSeconds);
    }
    step.startSeconds += end;
    return append(step);
}
inline std::optional<MotionSample> Motion::sample(double seconds) const {
    if (!std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(seconds) || seconds < 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    MotionSample result;
    for (const auto& step : steps_) {
        if (!step.enabled || seconds < step.startSeconds) continue;
        double time = seconds - step.startSeconds;
        if (step.durationSeconds) time = std::min(time, *step.durationSeconds);
        const auto matrix = step.transform(time);
        if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
        auto& target = step.space == TransformSpace::World ? result.parent : result.local;
        target = matrix * target; // Within each space, API order is application order.
        if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(target)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "Motion::sample", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(target)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    return result;
}
inline MotionStep Motion::fixed(const glm::mat4& matrix, TransformSpace space) {
    MotionStep step;
    if (TransformSystem::validAffine(matrix)) step.transform = [matrix](double) { return matrix; };
    step.space = space;
    step.durationSeconds = 0;
    return step;
}
inline MotionStep Motion::rotation(const RotationMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;
    if (!std::isfinite(config.degreesPerSecond) || !std::isfinite(config.initialDegrees)
        || !TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.initialDegrees)) return step;
    step.space = config.space;
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        const auto matrix = TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis,
            config.initialDegrees + config.degreesPerSecond * seconds);
        if (!matrix) throw std::domain_error("Rotation motion produced an invalid matrix.");
        return *matrix;
    };
    return step;
}
inline MotionStep Motion::followPath(const PathMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;
    if (!config.path || !config.interpolation || !std::isfinite(config.durationSeconds)
        || config.durationSeconds <= 0 || !std::isfinite(config.rate) || config.rate == 0
        || !std::isfinite(config.phase) || config.phase < 0 || config.phase > 1) return step;
    step.space = config.space;
    if (config.playback == Playback::Once) {
        step.durationSeconds = (config.rate > 0 ? 1 - config.phase : config.phase)
                             * config.durationSeconds / std::abs(config.rate);
        if (!std::isfinite(*step.durationSeconds)) return MotionStep{};
    }
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        double progress = config.phase + seconds / config.durationSeconds * config.rate;
        if (!std::isfinite(progress)) throw std::domain_error("Path time overflow.");
        if (config.playback == Playback::Once) progress = std::clamp(progress, 0.0, 1.0);
        else if (config.playback == Playback::Loop) progress -= std::floor(progress);
        else {
            progress -= std::floor(progress / 2) * 2;
            if (progress > 1) progress = 2 - progress;
        }
        const double u = config.interpolation(progress);
        if (!std::isfinite(u)) throw std::domain_error("Interpolation returned non-finite progress.");
        glm::vec3 offset = config.path(u);
        if (config.relativeToStart) {
            const double initial = config.interpolation(config.phase);
            if (!std::isfinite(initial)) throw std::domain_error("Invalid initial interpolation.");
            offset -= config.path(initial);
        }
        const auto matrix = TransformSystem::translation(offset);
        if (!matrix) throw std::domain_error("Path returned a non-finite position.");
        return *matrix;
    };
    return step;
}
inline MotionStep Motion::linear(const LinearMotion& config) {
    if (!detail::finiteVector(config.from) || !detail::finiteVector(config.to)
        || !std::isfinite(config.durationSeconds) || config.durationSeconds <= 0
        || (config.playback != Playback::Once && config.playback != Playback::Loop
            && config.playback != Playback::PingPong)
        || (config.space != TransformSpace::World && config.space != TransformSpace::Local)) return {};
    PathMotion path;
    path.path = [from = glm::dvec3(config.from), to = glm::dvec3(config.to)](double u) {
        return glm::vec3((1 - u) * from + u * to);
    };
    path.durationSeconds = config.durationSeconds;
    path.playback = config.playback;
    path.space = config.space;
    return followPath(path);
}
inline MotionStep Motion::pendulum(const PendulumMotion& config) {
    MotionStep step;
    if (!std::isfinite(config.amplitudeDegrees) || config.amplitudeDegrees < 0
        || !std::isfinite(config.periodSeconds) || config.periodSeconds <= 0
        || !std::isfinite(config.phaseDegrees) || !std::isfinite(config.offsetDegrees)
        || !std::isfinite(std::abs(config.offsetDegrees) + config.amplitudeDegrees)
        || (config.space != TransformSpace::World && config.space != TransformSpace::Local)
        || !TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis, config.offsetDegrees)) return step;
    step.space = config.space;
    step.transform = [config](double seconds) {
        constexpr double pi = 3.14159265358979323846;
        const double phase = std::remainder(seconds, config.periodSeconds) / config.periodSeconds * 2 * pi
                           + std::remainder(config.phaseDegrees, 360.0) * pi / 180;
        const auto matrix = TransformSystem::rotation(config.axis,
            config.offsetDegrees + config.amplitudeDegrees * std::sin(phase));
        if (!matrix) throw std::domain_error("Pendulum motion produced an invalid matrix.");
        return *matrix;
    };
    return step;
}
inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createMotion(const Motion& motion) {
    const auto sample = motion.sample(0);
    if (!sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample || nextMotion_ == std::numeric_limits<MotionId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const auto id = nextMotion_;
    MotionState state;
    state.motion = motion;
    state.sampled = *sample;
    motions_.emplace(id, std::move(state));
    ++nextMotion_;
    return id;
}
inline const MotionState* MotionSystem::getMotion(MotionId id) const {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);
    return found == motions_.end() ? nullptr : &found->second;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::setMotion(MotionId id, const Motion& motion) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);
    if (found == motions_.end()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotion", "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto sample = motion.sample(found->second.timeSeconds);
    if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.motion = motion;
    found->second.sampled = *sample;
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::setMotionPlaying(MotionId id, bool playing) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);
    if (found == motions_.end()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotionPlaying", "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.playing = playing;
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::setMotionEnabled(MotionId id, bool enabled) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);
    if (found == motions_.end()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setMotionEnabled", "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.enabled = enabled;
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::seekMotion(MotionId id, double seconds) {
    const auto found = motions_.find(id);
    if (found == motions_.end()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion", "Rejected condition: found == motions_.end()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto sample = found->second.motion.sample(seconds);
    if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::seekMotion", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.timeSeconds = seconds;
    found->second.sampled = *sample;
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::removeMotion(MotionId id) {
    if (!motions_.erase(id)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::removeMotion", "Rejected condition: !motions_.erase(id)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    for (auto& layer : layers_)
        if (layer.second.config.motion == id) layer.second.config.motion.reset();
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::update(double deltaSeconds) {
    if (!std::isfinite(deltaSeconds) || deltaSeconds < 0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::update", "Rejected condition: !std::isfinite(deltaSeconds) || deltaSeconds < 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    struct Pending { MotionId id; double time; MotionSample sample; };
    std::vector<Pending> pending;
    pending.reserve(motions_.size());
    for (const auto& entry : motions_) {
        const auto& state = entry.second;
        if (!state.playing) continue;
        const double time = state.timeSeconds + deltaSeconds;
        const auto sample = state.motion.sample(time);
        if (!sample) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::update", "Rejected condition: !sample", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
        pending.push_back({entry.first, time, *sample});
    }
    for (const auto& item : pending) {
        auto& state = motions_.at(item.id);
        state.timeSeconds = item.time;
        state.sampled = item.sample;
    }
    return true;
}
inline void MotionSystem::clear() {
    std::map<LayerId, LayerState> emptyLayers{{rootLayer(), LayerState{}}};
    motions_.clear();
    layers_.swap(emptyLayers);
}
inline bool MotionSystem::validLayer(const LayerConfig& config) const {
    return TransformSystem::validAffine(config.transform)
        && (!config.motion || getMotion(*config.motion));
}
inline const LayerState* MotionSystem::getLayer(LayerId id) const {
    const auto found = layers_.find(id);
    return found == layers_.end() ? nullptr : &found->second;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::pushLayer(LayerId parent, const LayerConfig& config) {
    if (!getLayer(parent) || !validLayer(config)
        || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::pushLayer", "Rejected condition: !getLayer(parent) || !validLayer(config) || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    auto staged = layers_;
    const auto id = nextLayer_;
    staged.emplace(id, LayerState{config, parent, {}});
    staged.at(parent).children.push_back(id);
    layers_.swap(staged);
    ++nextLayer_;
    return id;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::insertLayer(LayerId child, const LayerConfig& config) {
    const auto current = getLayer(child);
    if (!current || !current->parent || !validLayer(config)
        || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::insertLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !validLayer(config) || nextLayer_ == std::numeric_limits<LayerId>::max()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const auto parent = *current->parent;
    const auto id = nextLayer_;
    auto staged = layers_;
    staged.emplace(id, LayerState{config, parent, {child}});
    auto& siblings = staged.at(parent).children;
    std::replace(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), child, id);
    staged.at(child).parent = id;
    layers_.swap(staged);
    ++nextLayer_;
    return id;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::removeLayer(LayerId id) {
    const auto current = getLayer(id);
    if (!current || !current->parent) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::removeLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto staged = layers_;
    const auto parent = *current->parent;
    auto& siblings = staged.at(parent).children;
    const auto position = std::find(siblings.begin(), siblings.end(), id);
    const auto index = std::size_t(position - siblings.begin());
    siblings.erase(position);
    siblings.insert(siblings.begin() + index, current->children.begin(), current->children.end());
    for (auto child : current->children) staged.at(child).parent = parent;
    staged.erase(id);
    layers_.swap(staged);
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> MotionSystem::popLayer(LayerId top) {
    const auto current = getLayer(top);
    if (!current || !current->parent || !current->children.empty()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::popLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !current->children.empty()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const auto parent = *current->parent;
    if (!removeLayer(top)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::popLayer", "Rejected condition: !removeLayer(top)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    return parent;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::reparentLayer(LayerId id, LayerId parent) {
    const auto current = getLayer(id);
    if (!current || !current->parent || !getLayer(parent)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::reparentLayer", "Rejected condition: !current || !current->parent || !getLayer(parent)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    if (current->parent == parent) return true;
    auto ancestor = std::optional<LayerId>(parent);
    while (ancestor) {
        if (*ancestor == id) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::reparentLayer", "Rejected condition: *ancestor == id", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        ancestor = layers_.at(*ancestor).parent;
    }
    auto staged = layers_;
    auto& old = staged.at(*current->parent).children;
    old.erase(std::find(old.begin(), old.end(), id));
    staged.at(parent).children.push_back(id);
    staged.at(id).parent = parent;
    layers_.swap(staged);
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::setLayer(LayerId id, const LayerConfig& config) {
    const auto found = layers_.find(id);
    if (found == layers_.end() || !validLayer(config)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::setLayer", "Rejected condition: found == layers_.end() || !validLayer(config)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    found->second.config = config;
    return true;
}
inline bool MotionSystem::bindMotion(LayerId layer, std::optional<MotionId> motion) {
    const auto current = getLayer(layer);
    if (!current || (motion && !getMotion(*motion))) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::bindMotion", "Rejected condition: !current || (motion && !getMotion(*motion))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.");
    auto config = current->config;
    config.motion = motion;
    return setLayer(layer, config);
}
inline std::optional<MotionSample> MotionSystem::layerMatrices(LayerId leaf) const {
    std::vector<LayerId> chain;
    auto current = std::optional<LayerId>(leaf);
    while (current) {
        const auto layer = getLayer(*current);
        if (!layer || chain.size() >= layers_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !layer || chain.size() >= layers_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
        chain.push_back(*current);
        current = layer->parent;
    }
    MotionSample result;
    for (auto i = chain.rbegin(); i != chain.rend(); ++i) {
        const auto& config = layers_.at(*i).config;
        if (!config.enabled) continue;
        MotionSample own;
        if (config.space == TransformSpace::World) own.parent = config.transform;
        else own.local = config.transform;
        if (config.motion) {
            const auto state = getMotion(*config.motion);
            if (!state) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !state", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
            if (state->enabled) {
                own.parent = state->sampled.parent * own.parent;
                own.local = state->sampled.local * own.local;
            }
        }
        if (*i == rootLayer()) result.parent = own.parent * own.local;
        else {
            result.parent *= own.parent;
            result.local *= own.local;
        }
        if (!TransformSystem::validAffine(result.parent)
            || !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.local)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::layerMatrices", "Rejected condition: !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.parent) || !TransformSystem::validAffine(result.local)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createSpin(RotationMotion config) {
    config.space = TransformSpace::Local;
    Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    return createMotion(motion);
}
inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createOrbit(RotationMotion config) {
    config.space = TransformSpace::World;
    Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createOrbit", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(config))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    return createMotion(motion);
}
inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createOrbitAndSpin(RotationMotion orbit,
                                                            RotationMotion spin) {
    orbit.space = TransformSpace::World;
    spin.space = TransformSpace::Local;
    Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(Motion::rotation(orbit)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createOrbitAndSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::rotation(orbit)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    return createMotion(motion);
}
inline std::optional<MotionId> MotionSystem::createPathAndSpin(const PathMotion& path,
                                                           RotationMotion spin) {
    spin.space = TransformSpace::Local;
    Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(Motion::followPath(path)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Motion, "MotionSystem::createPathAndSpin", "Rejected condition: !motion.append(Motion::followPath(path)) || !motion.append(Motion::rotation(spin))", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    return createMotion(motion);
}
} // namespace graphics

모션 시계와 레이어를 연결·편집·해제하기

두 물체에 같은 경로 시계를 연결하되 한 분기만 반사시키는 경우다. MotionId는 시간 상태를 찾고 LayerId는 변환 합성 경로를 찾는다. 같은 모션을 공유하면 위상도 공유한다.

변수·객체 역할. system은 Scene 안의 MotionSystem이다. rootLayer는 삭제할 수 없는 0번 루트다. parent는 변환 트리 부모이며 motion의 World/Local 기여는 합성 위치를 나눈다.

새로 작성한 사용 사례 · 단편별 전제는 본문 참조 · 빌드하지 않음

bool connectMotion(graphics::Scene& scene,graphics::EntityId entity) {
    auto& system=scene.getMotions();
    const auto& constSystem=static_cast<const graphics::Scene&>(scene).getMotions(); (void)constSystem;
    graphics::Motion motion;
    graphics::RotationMotion rotation;
    if(!motion.append(graphics::Motion::rotation(rotation))) return false;
    auto id=system.createMotion(motion); if(!id) return false;
    if(!system.setMotion(*id,motion)||!system.seekMotion(*id,1)
       ||!system.setMotionPlaying(*id,false)||!system.setMotionEnabled(*id,true)) return false;
    const auto* state=system.getMotion(*id); if(!state) return false;
    auto layer=system.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer()); if(!layer) return false;
    graphics::LayerConfig config; config.motion=*id;
    if(!system.setLayer(*layer,config)||!system.bindMotion(*layer,*id)) return false;
    auto inserted=system.insertLayer(*layer); if(!inserted) return false;
    if(!system.reparentLayer(*layer,graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer())
       ||!system.removeLayer(*inserted)) return false;
    const auto* layerState=system.getLayer(*layer); (void)layerState;
    auto matrices=system.layerMatrices(*layer); if(!matrices) return false;
    if(!scene.bindEntity(entity,*layer)) return false;
    auto binding=scene.getEntityLayer(entity); (void)binding;
    if(!system.update(.016)||!scene.updateMotions(.016)) return false;
    // 위 두 호출은 수동 진행의 시연이다. Application 루프에서는 자동 진행하므로 호출하지 않는다.
    if(!scene.unbindEntity(entity)||!system.bindMotion(*layer,std::nullopt)) return false;
    if(!system.popLayer(*layer)||!system.removeMotion(*id)) return false;
    auto spin=system.createSpin(rotation);
    auto orbit=system.createOrbit(rotation);
    auto combined=system.createOrbitAndSpin(rotation,rotation);
    graphics::PathMotion path; path.path=graphics::PathSystem::circle({0,0,0},1);
    auto moving=system.createPathAndSpin(path,rotation);
    if(!spin||!orbit||!combined||!moving) return false;
    system.clear(); // 기존 레이어 연결을 사용하지 않는 별도 예제 종료 지점
    return true;
}

playing=false는 시간 정지, enabled=false는 변환 기여 제거다. popLayer는 자식 없는 레이어만 제거한다. removeLayer는 자식을 부모 쪽으로 승격한다. system.clear 전에 Scene 엔티티 바인딩을 해제해야 한다.

실제 저장소 소스 · standard/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <iostream>

/// 선택한 경로로 이동 모션을 바꾼다.
bool Console::choosePath(int kind) {
    graphics::PathMotion path;
    path.durationSeconds = 6;
    const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);

    if (kind == 1) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 2) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
    } else if (kind == 3) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polygon({
            {1, -.5f, 0},
            {2.4f, -.4f, 0},
            {2.1f, .6f, 0},
            {1.4f, .8f, 0},
            {.9f, .2f, 0}
        });
    } else if (kind == 4) {
        path.path = [center](double u) {
            const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;
            return center + glm::vec3(
                float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)),
                float(.6 * std::sin(angle)),
                float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
        };
        path.interpolation = graphics::PathSystem::smoothStep;
    } else if (kind == 5) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
    } else if (kind == 6) {
        path.path = graphics::PathSystem::polyline({
            {1, -.5f, 0},
            {2.4f, -.5f, 0},
            {2, .6f, 0}
        });
        path.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    graphics::Motion motion;
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    if (!pathId_ || !motion.append(graphics::Motion::followPath(path))) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!motions.setMotion(*pathId_, motion) || !motions.seekMotion(*pathId_, 0)) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "경로 " << kind << ": 시작 시각 0초로 변경\n";
    return true;
}

/// 장면 객체와 초기 상태를 구성한다.
bool Console::initScene() {
    demo_=Demo::Motion;
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    scene.clear();
    paused_ = false;
    spinEnabled_ = true;
    globalEnabled_ = false;
    reflection_ = 1;
    insertedLayer_.reset();

    const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
    if (!first || !second) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f})
        || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& motions = scene.getMotions();
    pathId_ = motions.createMotion(graphics::Motion{});
    graphics::RotationMotion spin;
    spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
    spinId_ = motions.createSpin(spin);
    graphics::RotationMotion global;
    global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
    globalId_ = motions.createOrbit(global);
    if (!pathId_ || !spinId_ || !globalId_ || !choosePath(1)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, false)
        || !motions.bindMotion(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId_)) {
        return false;
    }

    const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    const auto reflection = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    if (!normalPath || !reflection) {
        return false;
    }
    graphics::LayerConfig mirror;
    mirror.transform = *reflection;
    mirrorLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(graphics::MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);
    if (!mirrorLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer_);
    normalSpinLayer_ = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);
    if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);
    if (!mirroredSpin
        || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId_)
        || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId_)
        || !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer_, *spinId_)
        || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId_)
        || !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer_)
        || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first)
        || !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second)
        || !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) {
        return false;
    }

    auto& camera = app_.getCamera();
    camera.position = {5, 3, 8};
    camera.target = {0, 0, 0};
    camera.up = {0, 1, 0};
    printHelp();
    return true;
}

/// 반사 변환을 순서대로 변경한다.
bool Console::changeReflection() {
    const int next = (reflection_ + 1) % 4;
    std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);
    if (next == 1) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 2) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
    } else if (next == 3) {
        matrix = graphics::TransformSystem::reflectLine(graphics::AxisLine{});
    }
    if (!mirrorLayer_ || !matrix) {
        return false;
    }
    graphics::LayerConfig config;
    config.transform = *matrix;
    if (!app_.getScene().getMotions().setLayer(*mirrorLayer_, config)) {
        return false;
    }
    reflection_ = next;
    const char* names[] = {"없음", "YZ평면 반사", "원점 점대칭", "Y축 직선대칭"};
    std::cout << "반사: " << names[reflection_] << '\n';
    return true;
}

/// 크기 레이어를 삽입하거나 제거한다.
bool Console::toggleInsertedLayer() {
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    if (insertedLayer_) {
        if (!motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer_)) {
            return false;
        }
        insertedLayer_.reset();
        std::cout << "추가 크기 레이어 삭제\n";
        return true;
    }
    const auto scale = graphics::TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));
    if (!normalSpinLayer_ || !scale) {
        return false;
    }
    graphics::LayerConfig config;
    config.space = graphics::TransformSpace::Local;
    config.transform = *scale;
    insertedLayer_ = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer_, config);
    if (!insertedLayer_) {
        return false;
    }
    std::cout << "첫 객체에 1.5배 Local 크기 레이어 삽입\n";
    return true;
}

/// 키 입력에 따라 동작을 변경한다.
void Console::onKey(int key, int action) {
    if (action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_7 || key==GLFW_KEY_8 || key==GLFW_KEY_9 || key==GLFW_KEY_0) {
        const bool ok=key==GLFW_KEY_7?initGrid():key==GLFW_KEY_8?initRoom():key==GLFW_KEY_9?initAnimation():initScene();
        if(!ok) app_.requestClose();return;
    }
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_V) {threeViews_=!threeViews_;return;}
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_D) {trace_=!trace_;graphics::Diagnostics::configure({true,trace_});return;}
    if(demo_==Demo::Animation && handleJointKey(key)) return;
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_F && demo_==Demo::Room) {floorOpen_=!floorOpen_;return;}
    if(demo_!=Demo::Motion) {
        if(key==GLFW_KEY_SPACE) clock_.playing=!clock_.playing;
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) app_.requestClose();
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_H) printHelp();
        return;
    }
    auto& motions = app_.getScene().getMotions();
    bool ok = true;
    if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
        app_.requestClose();
    } else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) {
        ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
        const bool playing = paused_;
        ok = pathId_ && spinId_ && globalId_
            && motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId_, playing)
            && motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId_, playing)
            && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId_, playing);
        if (ok) {
            paused_ = !paused_;
            std::cout << (paused_ ? "시간 정지\n" : "시간 재개\n");
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
        ok = spinId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId_, !spinEnabled_);
        if (ok) {
            spinEnabled_ = !spinEnabled_;
            std::cout << "자전 기여: " << spinEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
        ok = globalId_ && motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId_, !globalEnabled_);
        if (ok) {
            globalEnabled_ = !globalEnabled_;
            std::cout << "전체 공전 기여: " << globalEnabled_ << '\n';
        }
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
        ok = changeReflection();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
        ok = toggleInsertedLayer();
    } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_H) {
        printHelp();
    }
    if (!ok) {
        std::cerr << "샘플 명령 적용 실패. 장면을 종료합니다.\n";
        app_.requestClose();
    }
}

/// 마우스 버튼 입력을 처리한다.
void Console::onMouse(const graphics::MouseEvent& event) {
    if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) {
        return;
    }
    std::optional<graphics::PickResult> hit;
    if(threeViews_) {
        const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();
        if(size[0]<3 || size[1]<2) return;
        const auto passes=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions());
        for(const auto& pass:passes) {
            const auto& v=pass.viewport;const double top=size[1]-v.y-v.height;
            if(event.framebufferX>=v.x && event.framebufferX<v.x+v.width && event.framebufferY>=top && event.framebufferY<top+v.height)
                hit=graphics::pick(app_.getScene(),pass.camera,event.framebufferX,event.framebufferY,{double(v.x),top,double(v.width),double(v.height)});
        }
    } else hit=app_.pickAt(event.windowX,event.windowY);
    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
              << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
              << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
              << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
              << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
              << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
              << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
              << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
              << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
              << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();demo_=Demo::Grid;clock_.reset();gridBoxes_.clear();
    graphics::GridLayout grid;grid.origin={-3,-.1f,-2};grid.extent={6,.2f,4};grid.cells={6,1,4};
    for(int z=0;z<4;++z) for(int x=0;x<6;++x) {
        const auto center=grid.cellCenter({x,0,z}),size=grid.relativeSize({.15f,1,.22f});
        if(!center || !size || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,*size,*center,(x+z)%2?graphics::GlColor{.25f,.3f,.4f}:graphics::GlColor{.4f,.45f,.55f})) return false;
    }
    if(!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f,.35f,-1.5f},{2.5f,.35f,-1.5f},{2.5f,.35f,1.5f}})) return false;
    for(unsigned i=0;i<5;++i) {
        const auto box=graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,{.4f,.5f,.4f},{0,0,0},{.25f+.14f*i,.7f-.1f*i,.3f});
        if(!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene,i+1,{-.07f,-.1f,.205f},.2f,{1,1,1},*box)) return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera()=graphics::Camera{};app_.getCamera().position={7,6,8};app_.getCamera().target={0,0,0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent=4;return true;
}
/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();demo_=Demo::Room;clock_.reset();floorOpen_=false;
    const auto room=graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene,{{-2,-1,-2},{2,2,2}});
    const auto cube=graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,{.5f,.5f,.5f},{0,-.75f,0},{1,.7f,.2f});
    if(!room || !cube) return false;room_=*room;roomCube_=*cube;
    roomBody_=graphics::RoomBody{};roomBody_.localPosition={0,-.75f,0};roomBody_.halfExtent={.25f,.25f,.25f};
    app_.getCamera()=graphics::Camera{};app_.getCamera().position={5,3,8};app_.getCamera().target={0,0,0};
    return true;
}
/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    scene.clear(); demo_ = Demo::Animation; clock_.reset(); animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear(); selectedJoint_=0; selectedAxis_=0;
    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f})) return false;
    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;
    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;
    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);
    if (!swing) return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;
    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));
    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);
    if (!person) return false;
    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();
    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose)) return false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;
        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30; sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;
        graphics::Motion motion;
        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway))) return false;
        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);
        if (!track) return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }
    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();
    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker)) return false;
    graphics::LinearMotion move;
    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2}; move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4; move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    graphics::Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move))) return false;
    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);
    if (!track) return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);
    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index=0;
        while(index<animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint!=id) ++index;
        if(index==animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed=graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene,id,graphics::Motion{});
            if(!fixed) return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index,name,animation_[index].rest}); return true;
    };
    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig,const std::string& prefix) {
        if(!control(rig.root,prefix+" root") || !control(rig.pelvis,prefix+" pelvis")
           || !control(rig.torso,prefix+" torso") || !control(rig.neck,prefix+" neck")) return false;
        for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
            const auto side=prefix+(i==0?" left ":" right ");
            if(!control(rig.shoulders[i],side+"shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i],side+"elbow")
               || !control(rig.hands[i],side+"hand") || !control(rig.hips[i],side+"hip")
               || !control(rig.knees[i],side+"knee") || !control(rig.feet[i],side+"foot")) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    if(!control(swing->frame,"swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge,"swing hinge")
       || !control(swing->seat,"swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person,"rider")
       || !personControls(*person,"standing")) return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10}; app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();
    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}
/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if(jointControls_.empty()) return false;
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_TAB) selectedJoint_=(selectedJoint_+1)%jointControls_.size();
    else if(key==GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) selectedJoint_=(selectedJoint_+jointControls_.size()-1)%jointControls_.size();
    else if(key==GLFW_KEY_X || key==GLFW_KEY_Y || key==GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_=key==GLFW_KEY_X?0:key==GLFW_KEY_Y?1:2;
    else {
        auto& track=animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved=track.rest;
        if(key==GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key==GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);axis[selectedAxis_]=1;
            track.rest.rotation=track.rest.rotation*glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),
                glm::radians(key==GLFW_KEY_LEFT?-5.0f:5.0f),axis));
        } else if(key==GLFW_KEY_UP || key==GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_]+=key==GLFW_KEY_UP?.05f:-.05f;
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_]+=key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP?.05f:-.05f;
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key==GLFW_KEY_MINUS || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow=key==GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;
            track.rest.scale=glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x*(grow?1.1f:1/1.1f),.1f,3.0f));
        } else if(key==GLFW_KEY_R) track.rest=jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else return false;
        if(!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(),animation_,clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest=saved;
            std::cerr<<"관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();return true;
}
/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if(jointControls_.empty()) return;
    const auto& control=jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest=animation_[control.track].rest;
    std::cout<<"joint "<<selectedJoint_+1<<'/'<<jointControls_.size()<<": "<<control.name
        <<" axis="<<"XYZ"[selectedAxis_]<<" position="<<rest.basis.x<<','<<rest.basis.y<<','<<rest.basis.z
        <<" pivot="<<rest.localBasis.x<<','<<rest.localBasis.y<<','<<rest.localBasis.z
        <<" scale="<<rest.scale.x<<'\n';
}
/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if(demo_==Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing) return;
    if(!clock_.advance(dt)) {app_.requestClose();return;}
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();bool ok=true;
    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if(demo_==Demo::Grid) {
        for(std::size_t i=0;i<gridBoxes_.size();++i)
            ok=graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene,gridBoxes_[i],distancePath_,clock_.seconds+distancePath_.length()*i/5)&&ok;
    } else {
        const float angle=.4f*float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix=glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),angle,glm::vec3(0,0,1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;roomTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix);roomTransform.basis=glm::vec3(matrix*glm::vec4(0,.5f,0,1));
        ok=scene.setTransform(room_,roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_,5,!floorOpen_)
            && graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_,{{-2,-1,-2},{2,2,2}},matrix,dt,floorOpen_,false,{0,-9.8f,0},{0,0,.4f*float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});
        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;cubeTransform.basis=roomBody_.position(matrix);
        if(!roomBody_.released) cubeTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok=scene.setTransform(roomCube_,cubeTransform)&&ok;
    }
    if(!ok) app_.requestClose();
}
/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if(!threeViews_) return app_.renderScene();
    const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();if(size[0]<3 || size[1]<2) return true;
    return app_.renderViews(graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions()));
}
실제 저장소 소스 · framework/examples/motion.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include <iostream>

int main() {
    using namespace graphics;
    Application app;
    std::optional<MotionId> pathId, spinId, globalId;
    std::optional<LayerId> mirrorLayer, normalSpinLayer, insertedLayer;
    bool paused = false, spinEnabled = true, globalEnabled = false;
    int reflection = 1;

    const auto choosePath = [&](int kind) {
        PathMotion path;
        path.durationSeconds = 6;
        const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);
        if (kind == 1) path.path = PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 2) path.path = PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 3)
            path.path = PathSystem::polygon({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0},
                {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
        else if (kind == 4) {
            path.path = [center](double u) {
                const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;
                return center + glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)),
                    float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
            };
            path.interpolation = PathSystem::smoothStep;
        } else if (kind == 5) path.path = PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
        else {
            path.path = PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
            path.playback = Playback::PingPong;
        }
        Motion motion;
        auto& motions = app.getScene().getMotions();
        return pathId && motion.append(Motion::followPath(path))
            && motions.setMotion(*pathId, motion) && motions.seekMotion(*pathId, 0);
    };

    app.setInitHandler([&](Application& current) {
        auto& scene = current.getScene();
        auto& motions = scene.getMotions();
        const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
        const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
        if (!first || !second) return false;
        if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) return false;
        pathId = motions.createMotion(Motion{});
        RotationMotion spin;
        spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
        spinId = motions.createSpin(spin);
        RotationMotion global;
        global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
        globalId = motions.createOrbit(global);
        if (!pathId || !spinId || !globalId || !choosePath(1)) return false;
        if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, false)
            || !motions.bindMotion(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId)) return false;

        const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
        const auto reflectionMatrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
        if (!normalPath || !reflectionMatrix) return false;
        LayerConfig mirror;
        mirror.transform = *reflectionMatrix;
        mirrorLayer = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);
        if (!mirrorLayer) return false;
        const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer);
        normalSpinLayer = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);
        if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer) return false;
        const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);
        if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer, *spinId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId)
            || !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer)
            || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) return false;
        if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first)
            || !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second)
            || !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) return false;
        current.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
        std::cout << "1 타원, 2 사각형, 3 다각형, 4 사용자 경로+보간, 5 원, 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
                  << "Space 시간 정지/재개, S 자전 표시, G 전체 공전, M 반사 변경, I 레이어 삽입/삭제\n"
                  << "M: 없음 -> YZ평면 반사 -> 점대칭 -> Y직선 대칭 순환. 클릭: 객체 ID. Esc 종료\n";
        return true;
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](Application& current, int key, int action) {
        if (action != GLFW_PRESS) return;
        auto& motions = current.getScene().getMotions();
        bool ok = true;
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) current.requestClose();
        else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
            paused = !paused;
            ok = motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId, !paused)
                && motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId, !paused)
                && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId, !paused);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
            spinEnabled = !spinEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId, spinEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
            globalEnabled = !globalEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, globalEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
            reflection = (reflection + 1) % 4;
            std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);
            if (reflection == 1) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
            if (reflection == 2) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
            if (reflection == 3) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectLine(AxisLine{});
            LayerConfig config;
            if (!matrix) ok = false;
            else { config.transform = *matrix; ok = motions.setLayer(*mirrorLayer, config); }
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
            if (insertedLayer) {
                ok = motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer);
                if (ok) insertedLayer.reset();
            } else {
                LayerConfig config;
                config.space = TransformSpace::Local;
                const auto scale = TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));
                if (!scale) ok = false;
                else {
                    config.transform = *scale;
                    insertedLayer = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer, config);
                    ok = insertedLayer.has_value();
                }
            }
        }
        if (!ok) current.requestClose();
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([](Application& current, const MouseEvent& event) {
        if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) return;
        const auto hit = current.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);
        if (hit) std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    });
    ApplicationConfig config;
    config.title = "Motion and layer tree";
    return app.run(config);
}

CPU 모델의 생성·형상 계약과 행렬 읽기

직육면체와 구·원뿔을 Scene에 등록하기 전에 형상과 변환을 확인한다. DiagramSystem은 GPU 자원 소유자가 아니라 CPU 모델 생성·계산 도구다.

변수·객체 역할. model.faces는 여러 면/메시다. transform은 SRT와 모델 피벗을 포함한다. modelMatrix는 T(basis)·R·S·T(-localBasis)를 계산한다.

새로 작성한 사용 사례 · 단편별 전제는 본문 참조 · 빌드하지 않음

void inspectModel(const graphics::Component_Poly& component) {
    auto cube=graphics::DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(graphics::GLShape::Cube,{1,1,1});
    auto sphere=graphics::DiagramSystem::makeSphere(.5f);
    auto cone=graphics::DiagramSystem::makeCone(.5f,1);
    bool valid=graphics::DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(cube);
    graphics::Transform3D transform;
    valid=graphics::DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)&&valid;
    auto matrix=graphics::DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(transform);
    if(!component.polyhedron.faces.empty()) {
        auto face=graphics::DiagramSystem::faceModelMatrix(component,component.polyhedron.faces[0]);
        (void)face;
    }
    (void)sphere;(void)cone;(void)valid;(void)matrix;
}

validPolyhedron은 면 구성의 유효성을, validTransform은 회전 기저와 수치의 유효성을 다룬다. 둘 중 하나의 성공이 다른 하나의 성공을 보장하지 않는다.

실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Scene.inl
#pragma once
#include <fstream>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>
#include <utility>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {

inline bool positiveLengths(const glm::vec3& value) {
    return finiteVector(value) && value.x > 0.0f
        && value.y > 0.0f && value.z > 0.0f;
}

inline std::uint64_t nextGeometryRevision() {
    // 장면 복사·교체 시에도 다른 형상에 같은 revision을 부여하지 않는다.
    // Scene과 Renderer는 한 렌더링 스레드에서 사용한다.
    static std::uint64_t revision = 0;
    return ++revision;
}

inline void appendFace(Polyhedron& polyhedron, GLFace shape,
                std::initializer_list<glm::vec3> vertices, GlColor color) {
    PlaneFigure face;
    face.shape = shape;
    face.localVertices = vertices;
    face.color = color;
    face.normal = glm::normalize(glm::cross(
        face.localVertices[1] - face.localVertices[0],
        face.localVertices[2] - face.localVertices[0]));
    polyhedron.faces.push_back(face);
}

} // namespace detail
using detail::finiteVector;
using detail::finiteMatrix;
using detail::validColor;
using detail::positiveLengths;
using detail::nextGeometryRevision;
using detail::appendFace;

inline Scene::Scene() {
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    polyId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    components_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    tetraFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 4);
    cubeFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 6);
    pyramidFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 5);
    lines_.reserve(MAX_LINES);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addGroup(const Transform3D& transform) {
    if(!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform))return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addGroup", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const auto id=components_.size();
    Component_Poly group;group.polyhedron.shape=GLShape::None;
    group.transform=transform;group.sideLengths=glm::vec3(0);group.transformOnly=true;
    components_.push_back(group);polyId_.push_back(id);
    geometryRevision_=nextGeometryRevision();return id;
}
inline bool Scene::setTint(EntityId id, GlColor color) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || !validColor(color))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTint", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !validColor(color)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    components_[id].tint=color;return true;
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addSphere(float radius,const glm::vec3& position,unsigned slices,unsigned stacks) {
    Transform3D transform;transform.basis=position;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeSphere(radius,slices,stacks),transform);
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCone(float radius,float height,const glm::vec3& position,unsigned slices) {
    Transform3D transform;transform.basis=position;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeCone(radius,height,slices),transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::push_entity(
    const Polyhedron& polyhedron, const Transform3D& transform
) {
    if (!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron)
        || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron) || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    glm::vec3 minimum = polyhedron.faces[0].localVertices[0];
    glm::vec3 maximum = minimum;
    for (const PlaneFigure& face : polyhedron.faces) {
        for (const glm::vec3& vertex : face.localVertices) {
            minimum = glm::min(minimum, vertex);
            maximum = glm::max(maximum, vertex);
        }
    }
    if (!finiteVector(maximum - minimum)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(maximum - minimum)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const EntityId id = components_.size();
    components_.push_back({ polyhedron, transform, maximum - minimum });
    polyId_.push_back(id);
    std::vector<FaceId>* faceIds = nullptr;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Cube) faceIds = &cubeFaceId_;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Tetra) faceIds = &tetraFaceId_;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Pyramid) faceIds = &pyramidFaceId_;
    if (faceIds) for (std::size_t i = 0; i < polyhedron.faces.size(); ++i)
        faceIds->push_back({id, i});
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return id;
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCuboid(
    const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, sideLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addRightTetrahedron(
    const glm::vec3& edgeLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Tetra, edgeLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addPyramid(
    const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Pyramid, sideLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceVisible(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, bool visible) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVisible", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].visible = visible;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceCullMode(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex,CullMode mode) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || faceIndex>=components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || (mode!=CullMode::Inherit && mode!=CullMode::None && mode!=CullMode::Back && mode!=CullMode::Front))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster,"Scene::setFaceCullMode","Invalid entity, face index or culling mode.","Check the model's face list and CullMode enum.",id);
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].cull=mode;
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
                               const glm::mat4& matrix) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || !finiteMatrix(matrix)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !finiteMatrix(matrix)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].localAnimationMatrix = matrix;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceVertexColors(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
                                const std::vector<GlColor>& colors) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    if (!colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: !colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    for (GlColor color : colors) {
        if (!validColor(color)) {
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: !validColor(color)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        }
    }
    face.vertexColors = colors;
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return true;
}

inline const Component_Poly* Scene::getComponent(EntityId id) const {
    return id < components_.size() ? &components_[id] : nullptr;
}

inline bool Scene::setPosition(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    // For a parented entity this position is in parent coordinates.
    if (id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setPosition", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    auto candidate = components_[id].transform;
    candidate.basis = worldPosition;
    return setTransform(id, candidate);
}

inline bool Scene::setSideLengths(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) {
    if(id>=components_.size()||!positiveLengths(sideLengths))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()||!positiveLengths(sideLengths)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto& c=components_[id];
    if(c.transformOnly)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: c.transformOnly", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=c.polyhedron;
    const auto box=detail::bounds(replacement);const auto oldSize=box.second-box.first;
    if(!positiveLengths(oldSize))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: !positiveLengths(oldSize)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto ratio=sideLengths/oldSize;
    if(!finiteVector(ratio))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(ratio)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    // Preserve topology/materials. Resize mesh coordinates around the model pivot.
    for(auto& face:replacement.faces) {
        for(auto& vertex:face.localVertices)vertex=c.transform.localBasis+(vertex-c.transform.localBasis)*ratio;
        face.transform.localBasis=c.transform.localBasis+(face.transform.localBasis-c.transform.localBasis)*ratio;
        recalculateNormals(face);
    }
    return setModel(id,replacement);
}

inline bool Scene::setParent(EntityId id, std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setParent", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto ancestor = parent;
    std::size_t remaining = components_.size();
    while (ancestor) {
        if (*ancestor >= components_.size() || *ancestor == id || remaining-- == 0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setParent", "Rejected condition: *ancestor >= components_.size() || *ancestor == id || remaining-- == 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        ancestor = components_[*ancestor].parent;
    }
    components_[id].parent = parent;
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::worldMatrix(EntityId id) const {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    std::vector<EntityId> chain;
    std::optional<EntityId> current = id;
    while (current) {
        if (*current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: *current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
        chain.push_back(*current);
        current = components_[*current].parent;
    }
    std::reverse(chain.begin(), chain.end());
    LayerId layer = MotionSystem::rootLayer();
    std::size_t anchor = chain.size() - 1;
    for (std::size_t i = chain.size(); i > 0; --i) {
        const auto binding = getEntityLayer(chain[i - 1]);
        if (binding) { layer = *binding; anchor = i - 1; break; }
    }
    const auto motion = motions_.layerMatrices(layer);
    if (!motion) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: !motion", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    glm::mat4 prefix(1), suffix(1);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < chain.size(); ++i) {
        auto& matrix = i <= anchor ? prefix : suffix;
        matrix *= detail::transformMatrix(components_[chain[i]].transform);
    }
    const auto result = motion->parent * prefix * motion->local * suffix;
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Scene::worldMatrix","World transform overflow after parent and motion composition.","Reduce coordinate/scale magnitudes and inspect the parent/layer chain.",id);return {};}
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::faceWorldMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex) const {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto world = worldMatrix(id);
    if (!world) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: !world", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    glm::mat4 child(1);
    if (face.independence)
        child = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), face.transform.localBasis)
              * detail::transformMatrix(face.transform);
    const auto result = *world * child * face.localAnimationMatrix;
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Scene::faceWorldMatrix","Face world transform overflow.","Inspect local face animation and entity transforms.",id);return {};}
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::lineWorldMatrix(LineId id) const {
    if (id >= lines_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::lineWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    if (lines_[id].owner) return worldMatrix(*lines_[id].owner);
    const auto global = motions_.layerMatrices(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    return global ? std::optional<glm::mat4>(global->parent * global->local) : std::nullopt;
}
inline bool Scene::bindEntity(EntityId id, LayerId layer) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::bindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_[id] = layer;
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::unbindEntity(EntityId id) {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::unbindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_.erase(id);
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> Scene::getEntityLayer(EntityId id) const {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return {};
    const auto found = entityLayers_.find(id);
    if (found == entityLayers_.end() || !motions_.getLayer(found->second)) return {};
    return found->second;
}

inline bool Scene::setTransform(EntityId id, const Transform3D& transform) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].transform = transform;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceTransform(
    EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
    const Transform3D& transform, bool independence
) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    face.transform = transform;
    face.independence = independence;
    return true;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addLine(
    const glm::vec3& start, const glm::vec3& end,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end)
        || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addLine", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end) || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    const LineId id = lines_.size();
    lines_.push_back({ start, end, color, owner });
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return id;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addVector(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    return addLine(origin, origin + vector, color, owner);
}

inline std::optional<std::array<LineId, 3>> Scene::addBasis(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::mat3& basisVectors,
    const glm::vec3& axisLengths, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(origin)
        || !positiveLengths(axisLengths)
        || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(origin) || !positiveLengths(axisLengths) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        if (!finiteVector(basisVectors[i])
            || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f
            || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])) {
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(basisVectors[i]) || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
        }
    }
    // 사전 검증 후 세 선을 함께 등록한다. 실패 입력은 장면을 변경하지 않는다.
    const std::array<GlColor, 3> colors{
        GlColor{ 1.0f, 0.2f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 1.0f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 0.4f, 1.0f }
    };
    std::array<LineId, 3> ids{};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        ids[i] = lines_.size();
        lines_.push_back({
            origin, origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i], colors[i], owner });
    }
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return ids;
}
